*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&  Include           ZHUIM_MAIN_2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
* ZHUIM_MAIN_2 is extension to ZHUIM_MAIN include, solely for size
*  purposes
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_2100  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_2100 INPUT.

  CLEAR : w_call_3100, w_exit.

  PERFORM check_text_status_initial.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      IF w_2100_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        IF w_2100_from = 8010 OR
           w_2100_from = 8090.
          SET SCREEN 0.
        ELSE.
          SET SCREEN w_2100_from.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        PERFORM free_emp_txt.
        PERFORM free_mgr_txt.
        PERFORM free_emp_txt1.
        PERFORM free_mgr_txt1.
        PERFORM free_ins_txt.
        PERFORM free_ntd_txt.
        PERFORM free_med_prac.
        PERFORM free_erg_txt.
        PERFORM free_erg_ass.
        PERFORM free_serv_txt.
        PERFORM free_serv_req.
        IF w_2100_action NE 'DISP'.
          PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
        ENDIF.
        IF w_2100_from = 3100.
          PERFORM call_3100 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                  zim_in_mast-incident.
        ELSEIF w_2100_from = 3200.
          PERFORM call_3200 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                  zim_in_mast-incident.
        ELSEIF w_2100_from = 5100.
          IF w_ima_name_old  IS NOT INITIAL.
            w_ima_name = w_ima_name_old.
          ENDIF.

        ENDIF.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'UPD3'.
      w_3100_action = 'UPD'.
      w_3100_from   = 2100.
      IF w_2100_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        CLEAR w_2100_changed .
        CLEAR w_code2.
        PERFORM free_emp_txt.
        PERFORM free_mgr_txt.
        PERFORM free_emp_txt1.
        PERFORM free_mgr_txt1.
        PERFORM free_ins_txt.
        PERFORM free_ntd_txt.
        PERFORM free_med_prac.
        PERFORM free_erg_txt.
        PERFORM free_erg_ass.
        PERFORM free_serv_txt.
        PERFORM free_serv_req.
        PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
        PERFORM call_3100 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.
      ELSE.
        w_call_3100 = abap_true.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'NOTES1'.
      IF w_2100_action = 'DISP'.
        w_call_act = abap_true.
      ELSE.
        w_call_act = ' '.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                               zim_in_mast-incident
                               'CASE'
                               ' '
                               w_call_act.
* Start of CR3197  DEVK9HRFUU
    WHEN 'NOTES9'.
      w_call_act = abap_true.
      PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                               zim_in_mast-incident
                               'DEPLOYMENT'
                               ' '
                               w_call_act.
* End of CR3197  DEVK9HRFUU

    WHEN 'DISPDOC'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_2100_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.
* Check If the Info Consent or IMP has been updated
      SELECT SINGLE imp_provided imp_date info_consent info_date
             FROM zim_in_mast
             INTO (zim_in_mast-imp_provided, zim_in_mast-imp_date,
                   zim_in_mast-info_consent, zim_in_mast-info_date)
             WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
               AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'NOTESALL1'.
      SUBMIT zhrim_notes_overview_im
             WITH p_pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
             WITH p_inc   = zim_in_mast-incident
             WITH p_rest  = abap_true
             WITH r_case  = abap_true
             WITH r_empc  = abap_true
             WITH r_empa  = abap_true
             WITH r_mgrc  = abap_true
             WITH r_mgra  = abap_true
             WITH r_ntdc  = abap_true
             WITH r_ntda  = abap_true
             WITH r_insc  = abap_true
             WITH r_mr    = abap_true
             WITH r_insa  = abap_true
             WITH r_meetf = abap_true
             WITH r_meeto = abap_true
             WITH r_meeti = abap_true
             WITH r_meets = abap_true
             WITH r_meetr = abap_true
             WITH r_meetc = abap_true
             WITH r_close = abap_true
      AND RETURN.

    WHEN 'SAVE'.
      PERFORM validate_2101.
      PERFORM validate_2102.
      PERFORM validate_2103.
      PERFORM validate_2104.
      PERFORM validate_2105.
      PERFORM validate_med_prac CHANGING w_error.
      IF w_error IS INITIAL.
        PERFORM save_contact_notes.
        PERFORM save_claim_details.
        PERFORM save_health_prof.
        PERFORM save_im_mast.
        PERFORM set_text_status_initial.
        PERFORM save_erg.
        PERFORM save_erg_notes.
        PERFORM save_service_request.
        PERFORM save_serv_req_notes.
        w_3102_refresh = abap_true.
        w_3104_refresh = abap_true.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Case has been successfully saved'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_call_3100 = abap_true.
        CLEAR w_2100_changed.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        PERFORM free_emp_txt.
        PERFORM free_mgr_txt.
        PERFORM free_emp_txt1.
        PERFORM free_mgr_txt1.
        PERFORM free_ins_txt.
        PERFORM free_ntd_txt.
        PERFORM free_med_prac.
        PERFORM free_erg_txt.
        PERFORM free_erg_ass.
        PERFORM free_serv_txt.
        PERFORM free_serv_req.
        PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
        PERFORM call_3100 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.
      ELSEIF w_exit = abap_true.
        IF w_2100_from = 8000.
          SET SCREEN 0.
        ELSE.
          SET SCREEN w_2100_from.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        PERFORM free_emp_txt.
        PERFORM free_mgr_txt.
        PERFORM free_emp_txt1.
        PERFORM free_mgr_txt1.
        PERFORM free_ins_txt.
        PERFORM free_ntd_txt.
        PERFORM free_med_prac.
        PERFORM free_erg_txt.
        PERFORM free_erg_ass.
        PERFORM free_serv_txt.
        PERFORM free_serv_req.
        PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
        IF w_2100_from = 3100.
          PERFORM call_3100 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                  zim_in_mast-incident.
        ELSEIF w_2100_from = 3200.
          PERFORM call_3200 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                  zim_in_mast-incident.
        ENDIF.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR w_2100_changed.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'UPD2'.
      w_1000_action = 'UPD'.
      w_1000_from   = 2100.
      PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
      PERFORM call_1000_a USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'DISP1'.
      w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
      w_1000_from   = 2100.
      PERFORM call_1000_a USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'P902'.
      p9001-pernr   = zim_in_mast-pernr.
      p9001-identnr = zim_in_mast-incident.
      PERFORM display_p902 USING 'O'.

    WHEN 'RESEND'.
      REFRESH r_cat.
      IF w_unsworn_claim = abap_true.
        PERFORM add_r_cat USING '03'.
      ELSEIF zim_in_mast-hod IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM add_r_cat USING '02'.
*        IF zim_incident-treatmentopt NE 1.
*          PERFORM add_r_cat USING '04'.
*        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM add_r_cat USING '01'.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM correspondence USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                 zim_in_mast-incident
                                 ' '
                                 abap_true.

    WHEN 'REM'.
      PERFORM call_7030_reminder.

    WHEN 'INA'.
      PERFORM display_userguide USING 'INA'.

    WHEN 'DISPREM'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_REMINDER_OVERVIEW'
        STARTING NEW TASK 'DISPREM'
        DESTINATION 'NONE'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_2100_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_tcode    = sy-tcode.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_2100  INPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_2102  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_2102 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'ACT1'.
      CLEAR w_popup_ucomm.
      w_contact_type = 'E'.
      w_contact_type_desc = 'Employee'.
      CALL SCREEN 8100 STARTING AT 5 6 ENDING AT 140 23.

  ENDCASE.



ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_2102  INPUT

  CASE w_code2.

      w_contact_type_desc = 'Employer'.
      CALL SCREEN 8100 STARTING AT 5 6 ENDING AT 140 23.

  ENDCASE.



ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_2103  INPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_2104  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_2104 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'ACT3'.
      CLEAR w_popup_ucomm.
      w_contact_type = 'I'.
      w_contact_type_desc = 'Insurer'.
      CALL SCREEN 8100 STARTING AT 5 6 ENDING AT 140 23.

    WHEN 'INS1'.
      PERFORM maintain_insurer USING 'I' '1'.

    WHEN 'INS2'.
      PERFORM maintain_insurer USING 'U' '1'.

    WHEN 'INS3'.
      PERFORM maintain_insurer USING 'I' '2'.

    WHEN 'INS4'.
      PERFORM maintain_insurer USING 'U' '2'.

  ENDCASE.



ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_2104  INPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_2105  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_2105 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'ACT4'.
      CLEAR w_popup_ucomm.
      w_contact_type = 'D'.
      w_contact_type_desc = 'Nominated Treating Doctor'.
      CALL SCREEN 8100 STARTING AT 5 6 ENDING AT 140 23.

    WHEN 'NTD1'.
      PERFORM maintain_health_professional USING 'I'
                              zim_in_mast-ntd.
      w_ntd_name = zim_med-name.

    WHEN 'NTD2'.
      PERFORM maintain_health_professional USING 'U'
                              CHANGING zim_in_mast-ntd.
      w_ntd_name = zim_med-name.

  ENDCASE.

  CALL METHOD med_prac_alv->check_changed_data.

  IF w_code2(4) = '%_GC' AND
     w_code2+9(2) = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_enter.
** Enter whilst in the ALV
** Unfortunately this doesn't work for change and enter at the same time
    PERFORM validate_med_prac CHANGING w_error.
    PERFORM refresh_med_prac_alv_total.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_2105  INPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_2200  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_2200 INPUT.

  CLEAR : w_call_3600, w_exit.

  PERFORM check_text_status_initial_pre.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      IF w_2200_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        PERFORM exit_2200_1.
        PERFORM exit_2200_2.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'UPD3'.
      w_3600_action = 'UPD'.
      w_3600_from   = 2200.
      IF w_2200_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        CLEAR w_2200_changed .
        PERFORM exit_2200_1.
        PERFORM call_3600 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.
      ELSE.
        w_call_3600 = abap_true.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'NOTES1'.
      IF w_2200_action = 'DISP'.
        w_call_act = abap_true.
      ELSE.
        w_call_act = ' '.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                               zim_in_mast-incident
                               'PRE88'
                               ' '
                               w_call_act.

    WHEN 'DISPDOC'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_2200_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_cm       = 'S'.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'NOTESALL1'.
      SUBMIT zhrim_notes_overview_scu
             WITH p_pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
             WITH p_inc   = zim_in_mast-incident
             WITH p_rest  = abap_true
             WITH r_case  = abap_true
             WITH r_empc  = abap_true
             WITH r_empa  = abap_true
             WITH r_mgrc  = abap_true
             WITH r_mgra  = abap_true
             WITH r_ntdc  = abap_true
             WITH r_ntda  = abap_true
             WITH r_insc  = abap_true
             WITH r_insa  = abap_true
             WITH r_mr    = abap_true
             WITH r_meeto = abap_true
             WITH r_leg   = abap_true
      AND RETURN.

    WHEN 'SAVE'.
      PERFORM validate_2201.
      PERFORM validate_2202.
      PERFORM validate_2203.
*      PERFORM validate_2104.
*      PERFORM validate_2105.
      PERFORM validate_3404a.
      PERFORM validate_med_prac CHANGING w_error.
      IF w_error IS INITIAL.
        IF zim_in_pre88-ass_status = 'I'.
          PERFORM notification_only_popup.
          w_exit = abap_true.
        ELSE.
          w_exit2 = abap_true.
        ENDIF.
        IF w_exit2 = abap_true.
          w_2000_refresh = abap_true.
          PERFORM save_claim_status.
          PERFORM save_contact_notes_pre88.
          PERFORM save_claim_details.
          PERFORM save_health_prof.
          PERFORM save_im_pre88.
          PERFORM set_text_status_initial_pre88.
          w_3102_refresh = abap_true.
          w_3104_refresh = abap_true.
          MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Case has been successfully saved'.
          IF w_call_3600 = abap_true.
            CLEAR w_2200_changed .
            PERFORM exit_2200_1.
            PERFORM call_3600 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                    zim_in_mast-incident.
          ELSEIF w_exit = abap_true.
            PERFORM exit_2200_1.
            PERFORM exit_2200_2.
            LEAVE SCREEN.
          ELSE.
            CLEAR w_2200_changed.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'UPD2'.
      w_1000_action = 'UPD'.
      w_1000_from   = 2200.
      CALL METHOD zcl_olims_class=>unlock_in_pre88
        EXPORTING
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
      PERFORM call_1000_a USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'DISP1'.
      w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
      w_1000_from   = 2200.
      PERFORM call_1000_a USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'P902'.
      p9001-pernr   = zim_in_mast-pernr.
      p9001-identnr = zim_in_mast-incident.
      PERFORM display_p902 USING 'O'.

    WHEN 'RESEND'.
      REFRESH r_cat.
      PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'P1'.
      IF zim_incident-treatmentopt NE 1 AND
         zim_incident-treatmentopt NE 10.
        PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'P2'.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM correspondence USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                 zim_in_mast-incident
                                 ' '
                                 abap_true.

    WHEN 'REM'.
      PERFORM call_7030_reminder.

    WHEN 'INA'.
      PERFORM display_userguide USING 'PRE88'.

    WHEN 'DISPREM'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_REMINDER_OVERVIEW'
        STARTING NEW TASK 'DISPREM'
        DESTINATION 'NONE'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_2200_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_tcode    = sy-tcode.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_2200  INPUT

                              zim_in_mast-ntd.

                              CHANGING zim_in_mast-ntd.

  IF w_code2(4) = '%_GC' AND

  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_2205  INPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3000  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3000 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      PERFORM free_case_act.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      CLEAR w_code2.
      LEAVE SCREEN.


    WHEN 'P--'.
      gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'P-'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      SUBTRACT 19 FROM gr_row_no-row_id.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P+'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      ADD 19 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab7 LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 19.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P++'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab7 LINES w_lines.
      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 18.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'UPD2' OR 'UPD1' OR 'UPD3' OR 'DISPDOC' OR 'UPDLEG' OR
         'CLOSE' OR 'DISP1' OR 'DISP2' OR 'DISP3' OR
         'DISP6' OR 'DISP7'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab7_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab7_row_no.

      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Case to be Processed'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Case'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        gr_outtab7_row_table = gr_row_table.
        w_3000_refresh = abap_true.
        IF w_code2 = 'DISP1'.
          w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
          w_1000_from   = 3000.
          PERFORM call_1000_a USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP2'.
          w_2100_action = 'DISP'.
          w_2100_from   = 3000.
          PERFORM call_2100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP3'.
          w_3100_action = 'DISP'.
          w_3100_from   = 3000.
          PERFORM call_3100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD2'.
          w_1000_action = 'UPD'.
          w_1000_from   = 3000.
          w_3000_refresh = abap_true.
          PERFORM call_1000_a USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD1'.
          w_2100_action = 'UPD'.
          w_2100_from   = 3000.
          w_3000_refresh = abap_true.
          PERFORM call_2100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD3'.
          w_3100_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3100_from   = 3000.
          w_3000_refresh = abap_true.
          PERFORM call_3100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPDLEG'.
          IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGR'.
            w_3200_action = 'UPD'.
            w_3000_refresh = abap_true.
          ELSE.
            w_3200_action = 'DISP'.
          ENDIF.
          w_3200_from   = 3000.
          PERFORM call_3200 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'CLOSE'.
          w_3300_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3300_from   = 3000.
          w_3000_refresh = abap_true.
          PERFORM call_3300 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISPDOC'.
          CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
            EXPORTING
              i_action   = 'U'
              i_pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
              i_incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP6'.
          w_4001_action = 'DISP'.
          w_4001_from   = 3000.
          PERFORM call_4001 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.


        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP7'.
          w_4000_action = 'DISP'.
          w_4000_from   = 3000.
          PERFORM maintain_depref  USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                         gr_outtab7-incident
                                         'D'.


        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.


  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3000  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3001  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3001 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      PERFORM free_case_act.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      CLEAR w_code2.
      LEAVE SCREEN.


    WHEN 'P--'.
      gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'P-'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      SUBTRACT 19 FROM gr_row_no-row_id.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P+'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      ADD 19 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab7a LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 19.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P++'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab7a LINES w_lines.
      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 18.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'UPD4' OR 'DISPDOC' OR
         'DISP1' OR 'DISP2' OR 'DISP3' OR 'DISP4'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab7a_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab7a_row_no.

      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Case to be Processed'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Case'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab7a INTO gr_outtab7a INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        gr_outtab7a_row_table = gr_row_table.
        w_3000_refresh = abap_true.
        IF w_code2 = 'DISP1'.
          w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
          w_1000_from   = 3001.
          PERFORM call_1000_a USING gr_outtab7a-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7a-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP2'.
          w_2100_action = 'DISP'.
          w_2100_from   = 3001.
          PERFORM call_2100 USING gr_outtab7a-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7a-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP3'.
          w_3100_action = 'DISP'.
          w_3100_from   = 3001.
          PERFORM call_3100 USING gr_outtab7a-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7a-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP4'.
          w_3400_action = 'DISP'.
          w_3400_from   = 3001.
          PERFORM call_3400 USING gr_outtab7a-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7a-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD4'.
          w_3400_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3400_from   = 3001.
          PERFORM call_3400 USING gr_outtab7a-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7a-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISPDOC'.
          CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
            EXPORTING
              i_action   = 'U'
              i_pernr    = gr_outtab7a-pernr
              i_incident = gr_outtab7a-incident
              i_cm       = abap_true.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.


  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3001  INPUT

      ADD 22 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab31 LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 22.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD mc2_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P++'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab31 LINES w_lines.
      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 21.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD mc2_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'UPD4' OR 'DISPDOC' OR
         'DISP4'.
      CALL METHOD mc2_sel_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab31_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab31_row_no.

      CALL METHOD mc2_sel_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Case to be Processed'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Case'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        gr_outtab31_row_table = gr_row_table.
        w_3000_refresh = abap_true.
        IF w_code2 = 'DISP4'.
          w_3400_action = 'DISP'.
          w_3400_from   = 3005.
          PERFORM call_3400 USING gr_outtab31-pernr
                                  gr_outtab31-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD4'.
          w_3400_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3400_from   = 3005.
          PERFORM call_3400 USING gr_outtab31-pernr
                                  gr_outtab31-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISPDOC'.
          CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
            EXPORTING
              i_action   = 'U'
              i_pernr    = gr_outtab31-pernr
              i_incident = gr_outtab31-incident
              i_cm       = abap_true.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.


  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3005  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3010  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3010 INPUT.
  DATA: l_ok_4001  TYPE c,
        l_mode     TYPE c,
        lv_count   TYPE i,
        ls_outtab7 TYPE gs_outtab_type7.

  CLEAR lv_count.
  CLEAR: l_mode.
  CLEAR w_4100_from.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      PERFORM free_case_act.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      CLEAR w_code2.
      LEAVE SCREEN.

    WHEN 'P--'.
      gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'P-'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      SUBTRACT 22 FROM gr_row_no-row_id.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P+'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      ADD 22 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab7 LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 22.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P++'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab7 LINES w_lines.
      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 21.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'UPD2' OR 'UPD1' OR 'UPD3' OR 'EDITNOTES' OR 'DISPDOC'
      OR 'UPDLEG' OR 'LEGREQ' OR 'CLOSE'  OR 'DISP1' OR 'DISP2'
      OR 'DISP3' OR 'DELDOC' OR 'UPD4A' OR 'UPD5' OR 'UPD6'
      OR 'DISP4A' OR 'DISP5' OR 'DISP6' OR 'DEPACT' OR 'DEPREF'
      OR 'DISP7' OR 'SAVE' OR 'UPD8' OR 'DISP8' OR 'UPD9' OR 'DISP9'.

      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab7_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab7_row_no.

      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0 AND w_code2 <> 'SAVE'.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Case to be Processed'.

      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1 AND w_code2 <> 'SAVE'.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Case'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        gr_outtab7_row_table = gr_row_table.
        w_3010_refresh = abap_true.

        IF w_code2 = 'DISP1'.
          w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
          w_1000_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_1000_a USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP2'.
          IF g_user_group = 'S'.
            w_2200_action = 'DISP'.
            w_2200_from   = 3010.
            PERFORM call_2200 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
          ELSE.
            w_2100_action = 'DISP'.
            w_2100_from   = 3010.
            PERFORM call_2100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
          ENDIF.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP3'.
          w_3100_action = 'DISP'.
          w_3100_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_3100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD2'.
          w_1000_action = 'UPD'.
          w_1000_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_1000_a USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD1'.
          IF g_user_group = 'S'.
            w_2200_action = 'UPD'.
            w_2200_from   = 3010.
            PERFORM call_2200 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
          ELSE.
            w_2100_action = 'UPD'.
            w_2100_from   = 3010.
            PERFORM call_2100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
          ENDIF.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD3'.
          w_3100_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3100_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_3100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD4A'.

          SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
                 WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
                   AND incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
          IF sy-subrc NE 0.
            CLEAR zim_in_mast.
          ENDIF.

          IF zim_in_mast-hod = abap_true.
            w_3700_action = 'UPD'.
            w_3700_from   = 3010.
            w_gao_task    = 'M'.
            w_gao_desc    = 'Update Certificate'.
            PERFORM call_3700 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
          ELSE.
            w_3400_action = 'UPD'.
            w_3400_from   = 3010.
            PERFORM call_3400 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
          ENDIF.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'SAVE'.
          PERFORM update_cm_incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD5'.
          w_3400_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3400_from   = 3010.
          w_3500_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3500_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_3500 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
*<<< A.ZORBA EPSS BRD 05
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD9'.
          w_3400_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3400_from   = 3010.
          w_3500_action = 'UPD'.
          w_4100_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3500_from   = 3010.
          w_4100_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_4100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP9'.
          w_3500_action = 'DISP'.
          w_3500_from   = 3010.
          w_4100_action = 'DISP'.
          w_4100_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_4100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
*>>>A.ZORBA EPSS BRD 05
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP4A'.
          w_3400_action = 'DISP'.
          w_3400_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_3400 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP5'.
          w_3500_action = 'DISP'.
          w_3500_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_3500 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPDLEG'.
          IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGR' OR
             sy-tcode = 'ZIM_CSA'.
            w_3200_action = 'UPD'.
          ELSE.
            w_3200_action = 'DISP'.
          ENDIF.
          w_3200_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_3200 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'CLOSE'.
          IF sy-tcode EQ 'ZIM_CSA' OR
             sy-tcode EQ 'ZIM_MDU'.
            w_3300_action = 'DISP'.
          ELSE.
            w_3300_action = 'UPD'.
          ENDIF.
          w_3300_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_3300 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'LEGREQ'.
          IF sy-tcode EQ 'ZIM_MDU'.
            w_call_act = 'D'.
          ELSE.
            w_call_act = 'U'.
          ENDIF.
          CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_LEG_REQ_OVERVIEW'
            EXPORTING
              i_action   = w_call_act
              i_req_area = 'L'
              i_pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr.
*             i_incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISPDOC'.
          IF sy-tcode EQ 'ZIM_IMA_DISP'.
            w_call_act = 'D'.
          ELSE.
            w_call_act = 'U'.
          ENDIF.
          IF g_user_group = 'S'.
            w_call_doc = 'S'.
          ELSE.
            w_call_doc = 'I'.
          ENDIF.
          CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
            EXPORTING
              i_action   = w_call_act
              i_pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
              i_incident = gr_outtab7-incident
              i_cm       = w_call_doc.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'EDITNOTES'.
          w_3011_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_3011 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DELDOC'.
          w_3012_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_3012 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DEPACT'.
          CLEAR: l_ok_4001.

          IF gr_outtab7-deploy_status = 'C'.
*            l_ok_4001 = 'N'.
*            MESSAGE i999(zz)
*              WITH 'Deployment Status is Closed.'.
          ELSE.
            SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_deploy
              WHERE pernr     = gr_outtab7-pernr
              AND incident    = gr_outtab7-incident.
            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              IF zim_in_deploy-case_accepted IS INITIAL.
                IF zim_in_deploy-place_type = 'SC' OR
                   zim_in_deploy-place_type = 'DC'.
                ELSE.
                  l_ok_4001 = 'N'.
                  MESSAGE i999(zz)
                    WITH 'The Case has not been accepted yet.'.
                ENDIF.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.

          CHECK l_ok_4001 IS INITIAL.
          IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_DEP' OR sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRDEP' OR
             sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGR'.
            w_4001_action = 'UPD'.
          ELSE.
            w_4001_action = 'DISP'.
          ENDIF.

          w_4001_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_4001 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.


        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP6'.
          w_4001_action = 'DISP'.
          w_4001_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_4001 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DEPREF'.
          w_4001_action = 'UPD'.
          w_4001_from   = 3010.

          CLEAR: zim_in_mast.
          SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
            WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
            AND   incident = gr_outtab7-incident.

          IF zim_in_deploy-deploy_status = 'C'.
            MESSAGE i999(zz)
              WITH 'Deployment Status is Closed.'
                   'Please use Display Deployment Referral.'.
          ELSE.

            CLEAR: zim_in_deploy.
            SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_deploy
              WHERE pernr     = gr_outtab7-pernr
              AND incident    = gr_outtab7-incident.

            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              l_mode = 'U'.
            ELSE.
              l_mode = 'I'.
            ENDIF.

            PERFORM maintain_depref
              USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                    gr_outtab7-incident
                    l_mode.
          ENDIF.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP7'.
          w_4001_action = 'DISP'.
          w_4001_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM maintain_depref  USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                         gr_outtab7-incident
                                         'D'.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP8'.
          w_3600_action = 'DISP'.
          w_3600_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_3600 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD8'.
          w_3600_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3600_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_3600 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3010  INPUT

*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3011 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      PERFORM free_edit_note.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      CLEAR w_code2.
      LEAVE SCREEN.


    WHEN 'P--'.
      gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      CALL METHOD edit_note_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'P-'.
      CALL METHOD edit_note_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      SUBTRACT 19 FROM gr_row_no-row_id.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD edit_note_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P+'.
      CALL METHOD edit_note_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      ADD 19 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab16 LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 19.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD edit_note_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P++'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab16 LINES w_lines.
      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 18.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD edit_note_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'EDITNOTES'.
      CALL METHOD edit_note_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab16_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab16_row_no.

      CALL METHOD edit_note_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Note to be Updated'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Note'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab16 INTO gr_outtab16 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        PERFORM call_notes_2 USING gr_outtab16-note_key
                                   ' '
                                   ' '
                                   ' '
                                   ' '.

      ENDIF.


  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3011  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3012  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3012 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      PERFORM free_disp_doc.
      IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGR' OR
         sy-tcode = 'ZIM_SIMA'.
        PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
      ENDIF.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      CLEAR w_code2.
      LEAVE SCREEN.


    WHEN 'P--'.
      gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      CALL METHOD disp_doc_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'P-'.
      CALL METHOD disp_doc_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      SUBTRACT 19 FROM gr_row_no-row_id.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD disp_doc_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P+'.
      CALL METHOD disp_doc_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      ADD 19 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab26 LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 19.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD disp_doc_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P++'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab26 LINES w_lines.
      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 18.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD disp_doc_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'DELDOC'.
      CALL METHOD disp_doc_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab26_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab26_row_no.

      CALL METHOD disp_doc_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Document to be Deleted'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Document'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab26 INTO gr_outtab26 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        PERFORM call_delete_document USING gr_outtab26-doc_seq_no.
        DELETE gt_outtab26 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The document has been'
                          'successfully deleted'.
        CALL METHOD disp_doc_alv->refresh_table_display.
      ENDIF.


  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3012  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3013  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3013 INPUT.
  DATA: lv_count_2 TYPE i,
        ls_outtab  TYPE gs_outtab_type7.

  CLEAR lv_count_2.
  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      PERFORM free_case_act.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      CLEAR w_code2.
      LEAVE SCREEN.


    WHEN 'P--'.
      gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'P-'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      SUBTRACT 22 FROM gr_row_no-row_id.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P+'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      ADD 22 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab7 LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 22.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P++'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab7 LINES w_lines.
      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 21.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'GAOMC' OR 'GAODEP' OR 'S49LVE' OR 'IPMAKEUP' OR 'SAVE'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab7_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab7_row_no.

      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0 AND w_code2 <> 'SAVE'.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Case to be Processed'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1 AND w_code2 <> 'SAVE'.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Case'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        gr_outtab7_row_table = gr_row_table.
        w_3013_refresh = abap_true.
        IF w_code2 = 'GAOMC'.
          PERFORM update_cm_incident.
          w_3700_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3700_from   = 3013.
          w_gao_desc    = 'Update Certificate'.
          PERFORM call_3700 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'SAVE'.
          PERFORM update_cm_incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'GAODEP'.
          w_3700_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3700_from   = 3013.
          w_gao_desc    = 'Update Dependent Declaration'.
          PERFORM call_3700 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'S49LVE'.
          w_3800_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3800_from   = 3013.
          PERFORM call_3800 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'IPMAKEUP'.
          w_3900_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3900_from   = 3013.
          PERFORM call_3900 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.


  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3013  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3014  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3014 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      PERFORM free_case_act.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      CLEAR w_code2.
      LEAVE SCREEN.


    WHEN 'P--'.
      gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'P-'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      SUBTRACT 22 FROM gr_row_no-row_id.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P+'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      ADD 22 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab7 LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 22.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P++'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab7 LINES w_lines.
      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 21.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'UPD2' OR 'UPD1' OR 'UPD3' OR 'DISPDOC' OR 'UPDLEG' OR
         'CLOSE' OR 'DISP1' OR 'DISP2' OR 'DISP3' OR
         'DISP6' OR 'DISP7' OR 'DISP8' OR 'UPD8'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab7_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab7_row_no.

      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Case to be Processed'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Case'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        gr_outtab7_row_table = gr_row_table.
        w_3014_refresh = abap_true.
        IF w_code2 = 'DISP1'.
          w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
          w_1000_from   = 3014.
          PERFORM call_1000_a USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP2'.
          IF g_user_group = 'S'.
            w_2200_action = 'DISP'.
            w_2200_from   = 3014.
            PERFORM call_2200 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
          ELSE.
            w_2100_action = 'DISP'.
            w_2100_from   = 3014.
            PERFORM call_2100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
          ENDIF.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP3'.
          w_3100_action = 'DISP'.
          w_3100_from   = 3014.
          PERFORM call_3100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD2'.
          w_1000_action = 'UPD'.
          w_1000_from   = 3014.
          w_3014_refresh = abap_true.
          PERFORM call_1000_a USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD1'.
          IF g_user_group = 'S'.
            w_2200_action = 'UPD'.
            w_2200_from   = 3014.
            w_3014_refresh = abap_true.
            PERFORM call_2200 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
          ELSE.
            w_2100_action = 'UPD'.
            w_2100_from   = 3014.
            w_3014_refresh = abap_true.
            PERFORM call_2100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
          ENDIF.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD3'.
          w_3100_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3100_from   = 3014.
          w_3014_refresh = abap_true.
          PERFORM call_3100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPDLEG'.
          IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGR'.
            w_3200_action = 'UPD'.
            w_3014_refresh = abap_true.
          ELSE.
            w_3200_action = 'DISP'.
          ENDIF.
          w_3200_from   = 3014.
          PERFORM call_3200 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'CLOSE'.
          w_3300_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3300_from   = 3014.
          w_3014_refresh = abap_true.
          PERFORM call_3300 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISPDOC'.
          IF sy-tcode EQ 'ZIM_IMA_DISP'.
            w_call_act = 'D'.
          ELSE.
            w_call_act = 'U'.
          ENDIF.
          CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
            EXPORTING
              i_action   = w_call_act
              i_pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
              i_incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP6'.
          w_4001_action = 'DISP'.
          w_4001_from   = 3014.
          PERFORM call_4001 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.


        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP7'.
          w_4000_action = 'DISP'.
          w_4000_from   = 3014.
          PERFORM maintain_depref  USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                         gr_outtab7-incident
                                         'D'.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP8'.
          w_3600_action = 'DISP'.
          w_3600_from   = 3014.
          PERFORM call_3600 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD8'.
          w_3600_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3600_from   = 3014.
          PERFORM call_3600 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.


  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3014  INPUT

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.

        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      ADD 22 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab40d LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 22.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.

      ENDIF.

      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 21.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD depref_lst_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'DISP1' OR 'DISP2' OR 'DISP3' OR 'DISPDOC' OR 'DISPLEG' OR
         'DEPREF' OR 'DEPACT' OR 'CLOSE' OR 'DISP6' OR 'DISP7'   OR
         'EDITNOTES' OR 'DELDOC'.    "CR3197  DEVK9HRFUU

      CALL METHOD depref_lst_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab40d_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab40d_row_no.

      CALL METHOD depref_lst_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING

        EXCEPTIONS

        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Referral to be Processed'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Referral'.
      ELSE.

        w_3016_refresh = abap_true.

          w_1000_action = 'DISP'.

                                    gr_outtab40d-incident.

          w_2100_action = 'DISP'.
          w_2100_from   = 3016.
          PERFORM call_2100 USING gr_outtab40d-pernr
                                  gr_outtab40d-incident.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISPDOC'.
          CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
            EXPORTING
              i_action   = 'U'
              i_pernr    = gr_outtab40d-pernr
              i_incident = gr_outtab40d-incident.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DEPACT'.

          CLEAR: zim_in_mast.
          SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_deploy
            WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab40d-pernr
            AND   incident = gr_outtab40d-incident.

          IF zim_in_deploy-deploy_status = 'C'.
            w_old_depstat = 'C'.
*            l_ok1_4001 = 'N'.
*            MESSAGE i999(zz)
*              WITH 'Deployment Status is Closed.'
*                   'Please use Display Activities.'.

          ENDIF.

          w_4001_action = 'UPD'.
          w_4001_from   = 3016.
          PERFORM call_4001 USING gr_outtab40d-pernr
                                  gr_outtab40d-incident.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP6'.
          w_4001_action = 'DISP'.
          w_4001_from   = 4000.
          PERFORM call_4001 USING gr_outtab40d-pernr
                                  gr_outtab40d-incident.

* Start of CR3197  DEVK9HRFUU
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'EDITNOTES'.
          w_3011_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_3011 USING gr_outtab40d-pernr
                                  gr_outtab40d-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DELDOC'.
          w_3012_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_3012 USING gr_outtab40d-pernr
                                  gr_outtab40d-incident.
* End of CR3197  DEVK9HRFUU

        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.
