      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Case to be Processed'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Case'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        gr_outtab7_row_table = gr_row_table.
        w_3020_refresh = abap_true.
        IF w_code2 = 'DISP1'.
          w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
          w_1000_from   = 3020.
          PERFORM call_1000_a USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP2'.
          w_2100_action = 'DISP'.
          w_2100_from   = 3020.
          PERFORM call_2100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP3'.
          w_3100_action = 'DISP'.
          w_3100_from   = 3020.
          PERFORM call_3100 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISPLEG'.
          w_3200_action = 'DISP'.
          w_3200_from   = 3020.
          PERFORM call_3200 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPDIMEP'.
          IF gr_outtab7-seq_no IS NOT INITIAL.
            PERFORM maintain_imep USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                        gr_outtab7-incident
                                        gr_outtab7-seq_no
                                        'U'.
          ELSE.
            w_3300_action = 'UPD'.
            w_3300_from   = 3020.
            PERFORM call_3300 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                    gr_outtab7-incident.
          ENDIF.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'CLOSE'.
          w_3300_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3300_from   = 3020.
          PERFORM call_3300 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISPDOC'.
          CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
            EXPORTING
              i_action   = 'U'
              i_pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
              i_incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.


  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3020  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3030  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3030 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      PERFORM free_case_act.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      CLEAR w_code2.
      LEAVE SCREEN.


    WHEN 'P--'.
      gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'P-'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      SUBTRACT 19 FROM gr_row_no-row_id.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P+'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      ADD 19 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab7 LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 19.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P++'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab7 LINES w_lines.
      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 18.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'DISPDOC' OR 'DISPLEG' OR 'CLOSE'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab7_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab7_row_no.

      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Case to be Processed'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Case'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        gr_outtab7_row_table = gr_row_table.
        w_3030_refresh = abap_true.
        IF w_code2 = 'DISPLEG'.
          w_3200_action = 'DISP'.
          w_3200_from   = 3030.
          PERFORM call_3200 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'CLOSE'.
          w_3300_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3300_from   = 3030.
          PERFORM call_3300 USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                  gr_outtab7-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISPDOC'.
          CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
            EXPORTING
              i_action   = 'U'
              i_pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
              i_incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'SENDMDU'.
      IF zim_screen-mdrp_date IS INITIAL.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        MESSAGE e999(zz) WITH 'The Medical Discharge Review'
                              'Panel Date must be entered'.
      ENDIF.

      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->check_changed_data.

      LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7
           WHERE sel_code = abap_true.
        EXIT.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Case to be sent to'
                              'the Medical Discharge Unit'.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM send_to_mdrp.
        w_3030_refresh = abap_true.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'The Selected Cases have been'
                              'sent to the Medical Discharge Unit'.
      ENDIF.


  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3030  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3040  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3040 INPUT.

  DATA         l_name(80)      TYPE c.

  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      PERFORM free_case_act.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      CLEAR w_code2.
      LEAVE SCREEN.


    WHEN 'P--'.
      gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'P-'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      SUBTRACT 22 FROM gr_row_no-row_id.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P+'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      ADD 22 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab7 LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 22.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P++'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab7 LINES w_lines.
      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 21.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'DISMDU' OR 'UPDMDU' OR 'APPEAL'.
      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab7_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab7_row_no.

      CALL METHOD case_act_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Case to be Processed'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Case'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        gr_outtab7_row_table = gr_row_table.
        IF w_code2 = 'UPDMDU'.
          PERFORM maintain_pre88_md USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                          gr_outtab7-md_seq_no
                                          'U'.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'APPEAL'.
          PERFORM maintain_pre88_md USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                          gr_outtab7-md_seq_no
                                          'A'.
        ELSE.
          PERFORM maintain_pre88_md USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                          gr_outtab7-md_seq_no
                                          'D'.
        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'INSMDU'.
      IF w_pernr IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'An Employee Number must be entered'.
      ELSE.

        PERFORM maintain_pre88_md USING w_pernr
                                        '0'
                                        'I'.
      ENDIF.


  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3040  INPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3100  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3100 INPUT.

  CLEAR w_call_2100.

  PERFORM check_text_status_activities.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
        IF w_3100_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
          PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
        ELSE.
          w_exit = 'I'.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        IF w_3100_from = 8000 OR
           w_3100_from = 8010.
          SET SCREEN 0.
        ELSE.
          SET SCREEN w_3100_from.
        ENDIF.
        PERFORM free_serv_txt.
        PERFORM free_rehab_txt.
        PERFORM free_erg_txt.
        PERFORM free_serv_req.
        PERFORM free_rehab_req.
        PERFORM free_erg_ass.
        PERFORM free_meet_sel.
        PERFORM free_mc_sel.
        PERFORM free_rtwp_sel.
        PERFORM free_mr_sel.
        PERFORM free_wel_sel.
        IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
          PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
        ENDIF.
        IF w_3100_from = 2100.
          PERFORM call_2100 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                  zim_in_mast-incident.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR : w_code2, w_3100_action.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'UPD1'.
      w_2100_from   = 3100.
      w_2100_action = 'UPD'.
      IF w_3100_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        CLEAR w_3100_changed .
        CLEAR w_code2.
        PERFORM free_serv_txt.
        PERFORM free_rehab_txt.
        PERFORM free_erg_txt.
        PERFORM free_serv_req.
        PERFORM free_rehab_req.
        PERFORM free_erg_ass.
        PERFORM free_meet_sel.
        PERFORM free_mc_sel.
        PERFORM free_rtwp_sel.
        PERFORM free_mr_sel.
        PERFORM free_wel_sel.
        PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
        PERFORM call_2100 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.
      ELSE.
        w_call_2100 = abap_true.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'NOTES1'.
      IF w_3100_action = 'DISP'.
        w_call_act = abap_true.
      ELSE.
        w_call_act = ' '.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                               zim_in_mast-incident
                               'CASE'
                               ' '
                               w_call_act.

    WHEN 'NOTESALL1'.
      SUBMIT zhrim_notes_overview_im
             WITH p_pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
             WITH p_inc   = zim_in_mast-incident
             WITH p_rest  = abap_true
             WITH r_case  = abap_true
             WITH r_empc  = abap_true
             WITH r_empa  = abap_true
             WITH r_mgrc  = abap_true
             WITH r_mgra  = abap_true
             WITH r_ntdc  = abap_true
             WITH r_ntda  = abap_true
             WITH r_insc  = abap_true
             WITH r_mr    = abap_true
             WITH r_insa  = abap_true
             WITH r_meetf = abap_true
             WITH r_meeto = abap_true
             WITH r_meeti = abap_true
             WITH r_meets = abap_true
             WITH r_meetr = abap_true
             WITH r_meetc = abap_true
             WITH r_close = abap_true
      AND RETURN.

    WHEN 'DISP2'.
      w_2100_from   = 3100.
      w_2100_action = 'DISP'.
      PERFORM call_2100_a USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'DISPDOC'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_3100_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.
* Check If the Info Consent or IMP has been updated
      SELECT SINGLE imp_provided imp_date info_consent info_date
             FROM zim_in_mast
             INTO (zim_in_mast-imp_provided, zim_in_mast-imp_date,
                   zim_in_mast-info_consent, zim_in_mast-info_date)
             WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
               AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'SAVE'.
      PERFORM validate_2101.
      PERFORM validate_3102e.
      PERFORM validate_3103.
      PERFORM validate_full_case_management.
*      PERFORM validate_2104.
*      PERFORM validate_2105.
*      PERFORM validate_med_prac CHANGING w_error.
*      IF w_error IS INITIAL.
*        PERFORM save_claim_details.
      PERFORM save_service_request.
      PERFORM save_serv_req_notes.
      PERFORM save_rehab.
      PERFORM save_rehab_notes.
      PERFORM save_erg.
      PERFORM save_erg_notes.
      PERFORM save_im_mast.
      PERFORM set_text_status_activities.
      w_3102_refresh = abap_true.
      w_3103_refresh = abap_true.
      w_3104_refresh = abap_true.
      CLEAR : zim_prov, zim_screen_serv_req, zim_screen_rehab,
              w_orgeh_name, zim_screen_erg.
      REFRESH: gt_outtxt6, gt_outtxt7, gt_outtxt8.
      MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Case has been successfully saved'.
*      ENDIF.
      IF w_call_2100 = abap_true.
        CLEAR w_3100_changed.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        PERFORM free_serv_txt.
        PERFORM free_rehab_txt.
        PERFORM free_erg_txt.
        PERFORM free_serv_req.
        PERFORM free_rehab_req.
        PERFORM free_erg_ass.
        PERFORM free_meet_sel.
        PERFORM free_mc_sel.
        PERFORM free_rtwp_sel.
        PERFORM free_mr_sel.
        PERFORM free_wel_sel.
        PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
        PERFORM call_2100 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.
      ELSEIF w_exit = abap_true.
        IF w_3100_from = 8000.
          SET SCREEN 0.
        ELSE.
          SET SCREEN w_3100_from.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        PERFORM free_serv_txt.
        PERFORM free_rehab_txt.
        PERFORM free_erg_txt.
        PERFORM free_serv_req.
        PERFORM free_rehab_req.
        PERFORM free_erg_ass.
        PERFORM free_meet_sel.
        PERFORM free_mc_sel.
        PERFORM free_rtwp_sel.
        PERFORM free_mr_sel.
        PERFORM free_wel_sel.
        PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
        IF w_3100_from = 2100.
          PERFORM call_2100 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                  zim_in_mast-incident.
        ENDIF.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR w_3100_changed.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'P902'.
      PERFORM display_p902 USING 'O'.

    WHEN 'REM'.
      PERFORM call_7030_reminder.

    WHEN 'DISPREM'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_REMINDER_OVERVIEW'
        STARTING NEW TASK 'DISPREM'
        DESTINATION 'NONE'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_3100_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_tcode    = sy-tcode.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3100  INPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3102  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3102 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'PROV1'.
      PERFORM maintain_provider USING 'I'
                                CHANGING zim_screen_serv_req-prov_code.

    WHEN 'PROV2'.
      PERFORM maintain_provider USING 'U'
                                CHANGING zim_screen_serv_req-prov_code.

    WHEN 'BODY1'.

        PERFORM add_email_body    USING '20'.
      ELSEIF zim_screen_serv_req-prov_activity = 'F'.
        PERFORM add_email_body    USING '21'.
      ELSE.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'A Provider Activity must be entered'.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM update_request_details_6.

  ENDCASE.

  CALL METHOD serv_req_alv->check_changed_data.

*&      Module  user_command_4102  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_4102 INPUT.
  CASE w_code2.
    WHEN 'SEL1'.
      IF zim_in_claim-stat  EQ 'C' AND
         zim_in_claim-reason_closure IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'Please provide the Reason for Closure'.
      ENDIF.
      IF zim_in_claim-stat  NE 'C'.
        CLEAR zim_in_claim-reason_closure.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'SAVE'.
      IF zim_in_claim-stat  NE 'C'.
        CLEAR zim_in_claim-reason_closure.
      ENDIF.
  ENDCASE.
ENDMODULE.                    "user_command_4102 INPUT

*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_4103 INPUT.
  CASE w_code2.
    WHEN 'SEL1'.
      IF w_review_type  EQ 'O' AND
         zim_in_req_rev-other_review_desc IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'Please provide a short description of the (other) review'.
      ENDIF.
    WHEN 'SAVE'.
      IF w_review_type IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Review Type must be entered'.
      ENDIF.
      IF zim_in_req_rev-begda IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date From must be entered'.
      ENDIF.
      IF zim_in_req_rev-endda IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Date To must be entered'.
      ENDIF.
  ENDCASE.
ENDMODULE.                    "user_command_4103 INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3107 INPUT.


  DATA lt_prim_incident   TYPE zim_in_prim_tab.

* primary claim
  PERFORM  get_prim_claim.

  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'MC1'.
      IF zim_in_mast-hod NE abap_true AND
         zim_in_mast-inc_type NE 'N'.



        IF gr_prim_claim->get_primary_claims_incident( ) IS INITIAL AND
           w_unsworn_claim EQ space.
          MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'There is no Primary Claim for this '
                            'incident. Please ensure certificate is '
                            'added to correct claim.'.
* end CR #1483 13/08/14

        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM maintain_mc USING 'I'
                                 0.

    WHEN 'CAP1'.
      IF zim_in_mast-hod NE abap_true AND
         zim_in_mast-inc_type NE 'N'.
        IF gr_prim_claim->get_primary_claims_incident( ) IS  INITIAL AND
           w_unsworn_claim EQ space.
          MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'There is no Primary Claim for this '
                            'incident. Please ensure certificate is '
                            'added to correct claim.'.
* end CR #1483 13/08/14
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM maintain_cap USING 'I'
                                  0.

  ENDCASE.

ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3107  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3200  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3200 INPUT.

  CLEAR w_call_2100.

  PERFORM check_text_status_legal.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
*      PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt_case2
*                           gt_outtxt_case_old
*                    USING  'CASE'
*                           'DISP'.
*      WHILE gt_outtxt_case[] = gt_outtxt_case2[].
*        message i999 with 'A Case Note must be entered'.
*        PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
*                                 zim_in_mast-incident
*                                 'CASE'
*                                 ' '
*                                 ' '.
*        PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt_case2
*                             gt_outtxt_case_old
*                      USING  'CASE'
*                             'DISP'.
*      ENDWHILE.
      IF w_3200_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        IF w_3200_from = 8030.
          SET SCREEN 0.
        ELSE.
          SET SCREEN w_3200_from.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR w_code2.

        PERFORM free_insa_txt.
        PERFORM free_leg_txt.
        PERFORM free_meet_sel.
        PERFORM free_wcrec_sel.
        PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'UPD1'.
      w_2100_from   = 3200.
      w_2100_action = 'UPD'.
      IF w_3200_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        CLEAR w_3200_changed .
        PERFORM free_insa_txt.
        PERFORM free_leg_txt.
        PERFORM free_meet_sel.
        PERFORM free_wcrec_sel.
        PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
        PERFORM call_2100 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.
      ELSE.
        w_call_2100 = abap_true.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'NOTES1'.
      IF w_3200_action = 'DISP'.
        w_call_act = abap_true.
      ELSE.
        w_call_act = ' '.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                               zim_in_mast-incident
                               'CASE'
                               ' '
                               w_call_act.

    WHEN 'NOTESALL1'.
      SUBMIT zhrim_notes_overview
             WITH p_pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
             WITH p_inc   = zim_in_mast-incident
             WITH p_rest  = abap_true
             WITH r_case  = abap_true
             WITH r_empc  = abap_true
             WITH r_empa  = abap_true
             WITH r_mgrc  = abap_true
             WITH r_mgra  = abap_true
             WITH r_ntdc  = abap_true
             WITH r_ntda  = abap_true
             WITH r_insc  = abap_true
             WITH r_mr    = abap_true
             WITH r_insa  = abap_true
             WITH r_inr   = abap_true
             WITH r_outr  = abap_true
             WITH r_meeti = abap_true
             WITH r_meets = abap_true
             WITH r_meetl = abap_true
             WITH r_meetc = abap_true
             WITH r_close = abap_true
             WITH r_email = ' '
      AND RETURN.

    WHEN 'DISP1'.
      w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
      w_1000_from   = 3200.
      PERFORM call_1000_a USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'DISP3'.
      w_3100_from   = 3200.
      w_3100_action = 'DISP'.
      PERFORM call_3100_a USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'DISPDOC'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_3200_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'LEGREQ'.
      IF w_3200_action = 'DISP'  OR
         ( sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGR' AND
           sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_CSA' ).
        w_call_act = 'D'.
      ELSE.
        w_call_act = 'U'.
      ENDIF.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_LEG_REQ_OVERVIEW'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_call_act
          i_req_area = 'L'
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'SAVE'.
      PERFORM validate_2104.
      PERFORM save_claim_details.
      PERFORM save_leg_notes.
      PERFORM save_im_mast.
      PERFORM set_text_status_legal.
      w_3202_refresh = abap_true.
      MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Legal Details have been'
                        'successfully saved'.
      IF w_call_2100 = abap_true.
        CLEAR w_3200_changed.
        PERFORM free_insa_txt.
        PERFORM free_leg_txt.
        PERFORM free_meet_sel.
        PERFORM free_wcrec_sel.
        PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
        PERFORM call_2100 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.
      ELSEIF w_exit = abap_true.
        IF w_3200_from = 8010.
          SET SCREEN 0.
        ELSE.
          SET SCREEN w_3200_from.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR w_code2.

        PERFORM free_insa_txt.
        PERFORM free_leg_txt.
        PERFORM free_meet_sel.
        PERFORM free_wcrec_sel.
        PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR w_3200_changed.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'P902'.
      PERFORM display_p902 USING 'O'.

    WHEN 'REM'.
      PERFORM call_7030_reminder.

    WHEN 'DISPREM'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_REMINDER_OVERVIEW'
        STARTING NEW TASK 'DISPREM'
        DESTINATION 'NONE'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_3200_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_tcode    = sy-tcode.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.


  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3200  INPUT

*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3300  INPUT

*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3300 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
*      IF w_3300_action NE 'DISP'.
*        PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt_case2
*                             gt_outtxt_case_old
*                      USING  'CASE'

*          PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt_case2
*                               gt_outtxt_case_old
*                        USING  'CASE'
*                               'DISP'.
*        ENDWHILE.
*      ENDIF.
      IF w_3300_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        IF w_3300_from = 8020.
          SET SCREEN 0.
        ELSE.
          SET SCREEN w_3300_from.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR w_code2.

        PERFORM free_imep_sel.
        PERFORM free_ftcax_sel.
        PERFORM free_ftc_sel.
        PERFORM free_md_sel.
        PERFORM free_depref.
        PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'NOTES1'.
      IF w_3300_action = 'DISP'.
        w_call_act = abap_true.
      ELSE.
        w_call_act = ' '.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                               zim_in_mast-incident
                               'CASE'
                               ' '
                               w_call_act.

    WHEN 'NOTESALL1'.
      SUBMIT zhrim_notes_overview
             WITH p_pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
             WITH p_inc   = zim_in_mast-incident
             WITH p_rest  = abap_true
             WITH r_case  = abap_true
             WITH r_empc  = abap_true
             WITH r_empa  = abap_true
             WITH r_mgrc  = abap_true
             WITH r_mgra  = abap_true
             WITH r_ntdc  = abap_true
             WITH r_ntda  = abap_true
             WITH r_insc  = abap_true
             WITH r_insa  = abap_true
             WITH r_mr    = abap_true
             WITH r_inr   = abap_true
             WITH r_outr  = abap_true
             WITH r_meeti = abap_true
             WITH r_meets = abap_true
             WITH r_meetl = abap_true
             WITH r_meetc = abap_true
             WITH r_close = abap_true
             WITH r_email = ' '
      AND RETURN.

    WHEN 'DISP1'.
      w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
      w_1000_from   = 3300.
      PERFORM call_1000_a USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'DISP2'.
      w_2100_from   = 3300.
      w_2100_action = 'DISP'.
      PERFORM call_2100_a USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'DISP3'.
      w_3100_from   = 3300.
      w_3100_action = 'DISP'.
      PERFORM call_3100_a USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'DISPDOC'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_3300_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'LEGREQ'.
      IF w_3300_action = 'DISP'  OR
         ( sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGR' AND
           sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_CSA' ).
        w_call_act = 'D'.
      ELSE.
        w_call_act = 'U'.
      ENDIF.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_LEG_REQ_OVERVIEW'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_call_act
          i_req_area = 'L'
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.


    WHEN 'SAVE'.
      PERFORM save_im_mast.
      WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.

      IF zim_in_deploy-pernr IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM save_zim_in_deploy_3300.
      ENDIF.

      MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Case Closure Details have been'
                        'successfully saved'.
      IF w_exit = abap_true.
        IF w_3300_from = 8010.
          SET SCREEN 0.
        ELSE.
          SET SCREEN w_3300_from.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR w_code2.

        PERFORM free_imep_sel.
        PERFORM free_ftcax_sel.
        PERFORM free_ftc_sel.
        PERFORM free_md_sel.
        PERFORM unlock_im_pernr USING zim_in_mast-pernr.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR w_3300_changed.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'P902'.
      PERFORM display_p902 USING 'O'.

    WHEN 'REM'.
      PERFORM call_7030_reminder.

    WHEN 'DISPREM'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_REMINDER_OVERVIEW'
        STARTING NEW TASK 'DISPREM'
        DESTINATION 'NONE'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_3300_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_tcode    = sy-tcode.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3300  INPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3302  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3302 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'PRE881'.
      PERFORM maintain_document USING '90'
                                      'PRE88'.

    WHEN 'CLOSERPT1'.
      PERFORM maintain_document USING 'Z4'
                                      'CLOSE'.

    WHEN 'PRE882'.
      CONCATENATE 'PRE88' '001' INTO w_doc_unique.
      PERFORM correspondence_send USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                        zim_in_mast-incident
                                        w_doc_unique
                                        ' '.

    WHEN 'CLOSERPT2'.
      CLEAR w_canc.
      CONCATENATE 'CLOSE' '001' INTO w_doc_unique.
      PERFORM correspondence_send USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                        zim_in_mast-incident
                                        w_doc_unique
                                        ' '.
      IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
        zim_in_mast-inc_status     = 'C'.
        zim_in_mast-closure_status = 'P'.
        PERFORM save_im_mast.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3302  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3303  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3303 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'PRD1'.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cert
             WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
               AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
*              AND category = 'F'.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'A Medical Certificate has'
                          'not been created for the employee'.
      ENDIF.

      PERFORM maintain_document USING '91'
                                      'PRD'.

    WHEN 'CLOSERPT1'.
      PERFORM maintain_document USING 'Z2'
                                      'CLOSE'.

    WHEN 'PRD2'.
      CLEAR w_canc.
      CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_EMPLOYEE_ENQUEUE'
        EXPORTING
          number = zim_in_mast-pernr
        IMPORTING
          return = rec_bapireturn1.

      IF rec_bapireturn1-type = 'E'.
        w_error = abap_true.
        MESSAGE ID     rec_bapireturn1-id
                TYPE   rec_bapireturn1-type
                NUMBER rec_bapireturn1-number
                WITH   rec_bapireturn1-message_v1
                       rec_bapireturn1-message_v2
                       rec_bapireturn1-message_v3
                       rec_bapireturn1-message_v4.
      ENDIF.

      CONCATENATE 'PRD' '001' INTO w_doc_unique.
      PERFORM correspondence_send USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                        zim_in_mast-incident
                                        w_doc_unique
                                        ' '.
      IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
        PERFORM create_it9203_pen.
        IF w_error IS INITIAL.
          zim_in_mast-inc_status     = 'R'.
          PERFORM save_im_mast.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'CLOSERPT2'.
      CLEAR w_canc.
      CONCATENATE 'CLOSE' '001' INTO w_doc_unique.
      PERFORM correspondence_send USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                        zim_in_mast-incident
                                        w_doc_unique
                                        ' '.
      IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
        zim_in_mast-closure_status = 'R'.
        PERFORM save_im_mast.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3303  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3400 INPUT.

* PERFORM check_text_status_legal.
  CLEAR w_exit.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      IF w_3400_action NE 'DISP'.
*        PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt_case2
*                             gt_outtxt_case_old
*                      USING  'WC'
*                             'DISP'.
*        WHILE gt_outtxt_case[] = gt_outtxt_case2[].
*          MESSAGE i999 WITH 'A Case Note must be entered'.
*          PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
*                                   zim_in_mast-incident
*                                   'WC'
*                                   ' '
*                                   ' '.
*          PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt_case2
*                               gt_outtxt_case_old
*                        USING  'WC'
*                               'DISP'.
*        ENDWHILE.
        IF w_3400_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
          PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
        ELSE.
          w_exit = 'I'.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        IF w_3400_from = 8050.
          SET SCREEN 0.
        ELSE.
          SET SCREEN w_3400_from.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR w_code2.

        PERFORM exit_3400.

      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'WCNOTES'.
      IF w_3400_action = 'DISP'.
        w_call_act = abap_true.
      ELSE.
        w_call_act = ' '.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                               zim_in_mast-incident
                               'WC'
                               ' '
                               w_call_act.

    WHEN 'IPNOTES'.
      w_call_act = abap_true.
      PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                               zim_in_mast-incident
                               'IP'
                               ' '
                               w_call_act.

    WHEN 'GAONOTES'.
      w_call_act = abap_true.
      PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                               zim_in_mast-incident
                               'GAO'
                               ' '
                               w_call_act.

    WHEN 'NOTESWCIPG'.
      SUBMIT zhrim_notes_overview_cm
             WITH p_pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
             WITH p_inc   = zim_in_mast-incident
             WITH r_wc    = abap_true
             WITH r_ip    = abap_true
             WITH r_epss  = abap_true
             WITH r_gao   = abap_true
      AND RETURN.



    WHEN 'DISP1'.
      w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
      w_1000_from   = 3400.
      PERFORM call_1000_a USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'DISP2'.
      w_2100_from   = 3400.
      w_2100_action = 'DISP'.
      PERFORM call_2100_a USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'DISPDOC'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_3400_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_cm       = abap_true.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'SAVE'.
      CLEAR : w_code2, w_error.
      PERFORM validate_3401.
      PERFORM validate_3404a.
      PERFORM validate_3405a.
      PERFORM validate_3411.
*     PERFORM validate_3412.
      PERFORM validate_3415.
      PERFORM validate_3416.
      PERFORM validate_3417.
      IF w_error IS INITIAL.
        w_ans = 'J'.
        IF w_amount_type = 'M' AND
           zim_in_rec_amt-begda  IS NOT INITIAL AND
           zim_in_rec_amt-amount IS INITIAL.
          PERFORM zero_amount_popup.
        ENDIF.
        IF w_ans = 'J'.
          PERFORM save_claim_status.
          PERFORM save_fixed_amount.
          PERFORM save_comparable_earnings.
          PERFORM save_piawe_details.
          PERFORM save_primary_claim.
          PERFORM save_pawe.
          PERFORM save_concurrent_claim.
          PERFORM save_external_claim_pc_details.
          PERFORM save_im_address.
          PERFORM save_cm.
          PERFORM save_ip_rec.
*         on close of an incident
          PERFORM action_status_close.
          MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Case Management Details have been'
                            'successfully saved'.
          IF w_exit = abap_true.
            IF w_3400_from = 8050.
              SET SCREEN 0.
            ELSE.
              SET SCREEN w_3400_from.
            ENDIF.

            PERFORM exit_3400.
          ELSE.
            CLEAR w_3400_changed.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'P902'.
      PERFORM display_p902 USING 'O'.

    WHEN 'REM'.
      PERFORM call_7030_reminder.

    WHEN 'DISPREM'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_REMINDER_OVERVIEW'
        STARTING NEW TASK 'DISPREM'
        DESTINATION 'NONE'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_3400_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_tcode    = sy-tcode.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'CMCORRO'.
      CLEAR w_code2.
      PERFORM validate_3401.
      PERFORM validate_3404a.
      PERFORM validate_3405a.
      PERFORM validate_3415.
      IF w_error IS INITIAL.
        PERFORM save_claim_status.
        PERFORM save_fixed_amount.
        PERFORM save_im_address.
        PERFORM save_cm.
        REFRESH r_cat.
        PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'DZ'.
        PERFORM correspondence USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                   zim_in_mast-incident
                                   ' '
                                   abap_true.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'UPDDIS'.
      CLEAR : w_new_disablement_date,
              w_dis_reason1, w_dis_reason2,
              w_dis_reason3, w_dis_reason4.
      CALL SCREEN 7040 STARTING AT 30 5.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3400  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3510  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3510 INPUT.

  REFRESH r_cat.
  CLEAR w_canc.

  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'IMPCLAIM'.
      CLEAR : zim_screen-ltr_category, w_canc.
      r_cat[] = g_update_d_range[].

      CALL SCREEN 8300 STARTING AT 45 10.

      IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
        CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_IMPORT_DOCUMENT'
          EXPORTING
            i_pernr       = zim_in_mast-pernr
            i_incident    = zim_in_mast-incident
            i_doc_unique  = 'IP001'
            i_cat         = zim_screen-ltr_category
            i_direction   = 'I'
          IMPORTING
            o_arch_object = l_obj.
      ENDIF.


    WHEN 'DISPCLAIM'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW_CAT'
        EXPORTING
          i_action        = w_3500_action(1)
          i_pernr         = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident      = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_cm            = 'I'
          i_cat_table     = g_update_d_range
          i_no_case_notes = 'X'.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'DISPEMPSTATE'.

      PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'DP'.
      PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'DQ'.
      r_cat_range[] = r_cat[].

      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW_CAT'
        EXPORTING
          i_action        = w_3500_action(1)
          i_pernr         = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident      = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_cm            = 'I'
          i_cat_table     = r_cat_range
          i_no_case_notes = 'X'.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'DISPMEMSTATE'.
      PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'DR'.
      PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'DT'.
      PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'DS'.
      PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'DU'.
      r_cat_range[] = r_cat[].

      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW_CAT'
        EXPORTING
          i_action        = w_3500_action(1)
          i_pernr         = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident      = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_cm            = 'I'
          i_cat_table     = r_cat_range
          i_no_case_notes = 'X'.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.


    WHEN 'SENDEMPSTATE'.
*      PERFORM validate_3401.
*      PERFORM validate_3404a.
      IF zim_in_cm-ip_type IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Income Protection Type'
                          'must be entered before the Employers'
                          'Statement can be sent'.
      ELSEIF zim_in_cm-ip_type = 'S'.
        PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'DP'.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'DQ'.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM correspondence USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                   zim_in_mast-incident
                                   'LDAW'
                                   abap_true.
      IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
        zim_in_cm-emp_sent_date = sy-datum.
        PERFORM save_cm.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Case Management Details have been'
                          'successfully saved'.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'SENDMEMSTATE'.
      IF zim_in_cm-ip_type IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Income Protection Type'
                          'must be entered before the Members'
                          'Statement can be sent'.
      ELSEIF zim_in_cm-duty_det IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Duty Determination'
                          'must be entered before the Members'
                          'Statement can be sent'.
      ELSEIF zim_in_cm-ip_type = 'S'.
        IF zim_in_cm-duty_det ='O'.
          PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'DR'.
        ELSE.
          PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'DT'.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        IF zim_in_cm-duty_det ='O'.
          PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'DS'.
        ELSE.
          PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'DU'.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM correspondence USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                   zim_in_mast-incident
                                   ' '
                                   abap_true.

    WHEN 'SENDCLAIM'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW_CAT'
        EXPORTING
          i_action        = w_3500_action(1)
          i_pernr         = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident      = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_cm            = 'I'
          i_cat_table     = g_update_d_range
          i_no_case_notes = 'X'.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'CHECKLIST'.
      PERFORM maintain_document USING 'DX'
                                      'IPCHECKLIST'.


  ENDCASE.

ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3510  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3600  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3600 INPUT.

  CLEAR : w_call_2100, w_exit.

* PERFORM check_text_status_activities.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      IF w_3600_action NE 'DISP'.
        IF w_3600_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
          PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
        ELSE.
          w_exit = 'I'.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        PERFORM exit_3600_1.
        IF w_3600_from = 2200.
          PERFORM call_2200 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                  zim_in_mast-incident.
        ENDIF.
        IF w_3600_2_from = 3600.
          CLEAR w_3600_2_from.
          PERFORM call_3600 USING w_3600_saved_pernr
                                  w_3600_saved_incident.
        ENDIF.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'UPD1'.
      w_2200_from   = 3600.
      w_2200_action = 'UPD'.
      IF w_3600_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        PERFORM exit_3600_1.
        PERFORM call_2200 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.
      ELSE.
        w_call_2200 = abap_true.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'NOTES1'.
      IF w_3600_action = 'DISP'.
        w_call_act = abap_true.
      ELSE.
        w_call_act = ' '.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                               zim_in_mast-incident
                               'PRE88'
                               ' '
                               w_call_act.

    WHEN 'NOTESALL1'.
      SUBMIT zhrim_notes_overview_scu
             WITH p_pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
             WITH p_inc   = zim_in_mast-incident
             WITH p_rest  = abap_true
             WITH r_case  = abap_true
             WITH r_empc  = abap_true
             WITH r_empa  = abap_true
             WITH r_mgrc  = abap_true
             WITH r_mgra  = abap_true
             WITH r_ntdc  = abap_true
             WITH r_ntda  = abap_true
             WITH r_insc  = abap_true
             WITH r_mr    = abap_true
             WITH r_insa  = abap_true
             WITH r_meeto = abap_true
             WITH r_leg   = abap_true
      AND RETURN.

    WHEN 'DISP1'.
      w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
      w_1000_from   = 3600.
      PERFORM call_1000_a USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'DISP2'.
      w_2200_from   = 3600.
      w_2200_action = 'DISP'.
      PERFORM call_2200_a USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'DISPDOC'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_3600_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_cm       = 'S'.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'SAVE'.
      PERFORM validate_2201.
      PERFORM validate_3601.
      PERFORM validate_3608.
      PERFORM save_claim_status.
      PERFORM save_im_pre88.
*     PERFORM set_text_status_activities.
      w_3602_refresh = abap_true.
      MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Case has been successfully saved'.
*      ENDIF.
      IF w_call_2200 = abap_true.
        PERFORM exit_3600_1.
        PERFORM call_2200 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.
      ELSEIF w_exit = abap_true.
        PERFORM exit_3600_1.
        IF w_3600_from = 2200.
          PERFORM call_2200 USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                  zim_in_mast-incident.
        ENDIF.
        IF w_3600_2_from = 3600.
          CLEAR w_3600_2_from.
          PERFORM call_3600 USING w_3600_saved_pernr
                                  w_3600_saved_incident.
        ENDIF.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR w_3600_changed.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'P902'.
      PERFORM display_p902 USING 'O'.

    WHEN 'REM'.
      PERFORM call_7030_reminder.

    WHEN 'DISPREM'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_REMINDER_OVERVIEW'
        STARTING NEW TASK 'DISPREM'
        DESTINATION 'NONE'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_3600_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_tcode    = sy-tcode.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

* This code has not been included on screen 3608 to allow
* the screen to be exited immediately.
    WHEN 'RET2'.

      IF zim_in_pre88-rec_comp IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Leter has not been Completed'.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM return_recommendation.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3600  INPUT

  CASE w_code2.

                                  0
                                  'I'.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3608  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3608 INPUT.


  CASE w_code2.


    WHEN 'FILE1'.

      PERFORM maintain_document USING 'PD'
                                      'PRE88FN'.
    WHEN 'REC1'.

      IF zim_in_pre88-recommendation = 'A'.
        PERFORM maintain_document USING 'P6'
                                        'PRE88REC'.
      ELSEIF zim_in_pre88-recommendation = 'D'.
        PERFORM maintain_document USING 'P5'
                                        'PRE88REC'.
      ELSEIF zim_in_pre88-recommendation IS INITIAL.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'A Recommendation must be entered'
                          'before the Letter can be Updated'.
      ENDIF.
    WHEN 'LIAB1'.

      IF zim_in_pre88-recommendation = 'A'.
        PERFORM maintain_document USING 'P8'
                                        'PRE88ACC'.
      ELSEIF zim_in_pre88-recommendation = 'D'.
        PERFORM maintain_document USING 'P7'
                                        'PRE88ACC'.
      ELSEIF zim_in_pre88-recommendation IS INITIAL.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'A Recommendation must be entered'
                          'before the Accept/Decline Letter'
                          'can be Updated'.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'LIAB2'.

      IF zim_in_pre88-rec_app_dir IS INITIAL.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Notice of Liability cannot'
                          'be sent until it has been Approved'
                          'by the Director'.
      ELSEIF zim_in_pre88-decline_imp_date IS INITIAL AND
             zim_in_pre88-recommendation = 'D'.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Notice of Liability cannot'
                          'be sent until the Signed Decline'
                          'Letter has been imported'.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM send_liab_email.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'IMP1'.

      IF zim_in_pre88-rec_app_dir IS INITIAL.
        CLEAR w_ucomm.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Director Review must be Complete'
                          'before the Signed Decline letter'
                          'can be imported'.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM upload_doc USING 'PRE88ACC'
                                 '001'
                                 'PE'.
        IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
          zim_in_pre88-decline_imp_date = sy-datum.
          zim_in_pre88-current_status   = 'C'.
          zim_in_pre88-current_action   = '34'.
          zim_in_pre88-status_date      = sy-datum.
          PERFORM save_im_pre88.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3608  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3800  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3800 INPUT.

* PERFORM check_text_status_legal.
  CLEAR w_exit.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      IF w_3800_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        IF w_3800_from = 8030.
          SET SCREEN 0.
        ELSE.
          SET SCREEN w_3800_from.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR w_code2.

        PERFORM free_mc_sel.
        PERFORM free_clm_sel.
        PERFORM free_s49_sel.
        PERFORM free_wc_lve.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'WCNOTES'.
      IF w_3800_action = 'DISP'.
        w_call_act = abap_true.
      ELSE.
        w_call_act = ' '.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                               zim_in_mast-incident
                               'WC'
                               ' '
                               w_call_act.

    WHEN 'DISPDOC'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_3800_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_cm       = abap_true.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'SAVE'.
      CLEAR : w_code2, w_error.
      PERFORM validate_3909a.
      IF zim_screen-s49_numbr = 0.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Section 49 Leave'
                          'cannot be requested'.
      ENDIF.
** start CR 1694
*      IF pa0001-persk BETWEEN 'XA' AND 'XF'.   "Police
*        READ TABLE gt_outtab38 INTO gr_outtab38
*          WITH KEY subty = '5200'.
*        IF sy-subrc = 0.
*          IF zim_screen-s49_begda BETWEEN
*            gr_outtab38-begda AND gr_outtab38-endda.
*            MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Cannot create S49-'
*                          'incorrect leave code'.
*          ENDIF.
*          IF zim_screen-s49_endda BETWEEN
*            gr_outtab38-begda AND gr_outtab38-endda.
*            MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Cannot create S49-'
*                          'incorrect leave code'.
*          ENDIF.
*
*        ENDIF.
*      ENDIF.
** end CR 1694

      PERFORM save_s49_leave.
      IF w_error IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Section 49 Leave has been'
                          'successfully saved'.
        IF w_exit = abap_true.
          IF w_3800_from = 8010.
            SET SCREEN 0.
          ELSE.
            SET SCREEN w_3800_from.
          ENDIF.

          PERFORM free_mc_sel.
          PERFORM free_clm_sel.
          PERFORM free_s49_sel.
          PERFORM free_wc_lve.
          LEAVE SCREEN.
        ELSE.
          CLEAR w_3800_changed.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'REM'.
      PERFORM call_7030_reminder.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3800  INPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3900  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3900 INPUT.

* PERFORM check_text_status_legal.
  CLEAR w_exit.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      IF w_3900_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        IF w_3900_from = 8030.
          SET SCREEN 0.
        ELSE.
          SET SCREEN w_3900_from.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR w_code2.

        PERFORM free_mc_sel.
        PERFORM free_clm_sel.
        PERFORM free_s49_sel.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'IPNOTES'.
      IF w_3900_action = 'DISP'.
        w_call_act = abap_true.
      ELSE.
        w_call_act = ' '.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                               zim_in_mast-incident
                               'IP'
                               ' '
                               w_call_act.

    WHEN 'DISPDOC'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_3900_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_cm       = abap_true.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'SAVE'.
      CLEAR : w_code2, w_error.
      PERFORM validate_3909a.
      IF zim_screen-s49_numbr = 0.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'An IP Make-Up value'
                          'must be entered'.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM save_ip_leave.
      IF w_error IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Police IP Make-Up Leave has been'
                          'successfully saved'.
        IF w_exit = abap_true.
          IF w_3900_from = 8010.
            SET SCREEN 0.
          ELSE.
            SET SCREEN w_3900_from.
          ENDIF.

          PERFORM free_mc_sel.
          PERFORM free_clm_sel.
          PERFORM free_s49_sel.
          LEAVE SCREEN.
        ELSE.
          CLEAR w_3900_changed.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'REM'.
      PERFORM call_7030_reminder.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3900  INPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_5000  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_5000 INPUT.


  CALL METHOD erg_ass_alv->check_changed_data.

  IF w_5000_refresh = abap_true.
    IF gt_outtab10[] NE gt_outtab10_old[].
      PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      IF w_exit IS INITIAL.
        w_ima      = w_ima_old.
        w_ima_name = w_ima_name_old.
        CLEAR w_5000_refresh.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR w_exit.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      IF gt_outtab10[] NE gt_outtab10_old[].
        PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        PERFORM free_erg_ass.
        SET SCREEN 0.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.


  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'P--'.
      gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      CALL METHOD erg_ass_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'P-'.
      CALL METHOD erg_ass_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      SUBTRACT 19 FROM gr_row_no-row_id.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD erg_ass_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P+'.
      CALL METHOD erg_ass_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      ADD 19 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab10 LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 19.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD erg_ass_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P++'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab10 LINES w_lines.
      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 18.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD erg_ass_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'SAVE'.
      PERFORM validate_erg_ass CHANGING w_error.
      IF w_error IS INITIAL.
        PERFORM save_erg_2.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Ergonomic Assessments'
                          'have been successfully saved'.
        IF w_exit = abap_true.
          PERFORM free_erg_ass.
          SET SCREEN 0.
          CLEAR w_code2.
          LEAVE SCREEN.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  IF w_code2(4) = '%_GC' AND
     w_code2+9(2) = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_enter.
** Enter whilst in the ALV
** Unfortunately this doesn't work for change and enter at the same time
    PERFORM validate_erg_ass CHANGING w_error.
    PERFORM refresh_erg_ass_alv_total.
  ENDIF.



  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_5000  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_5100  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_5100 INPUT.
  DATA: l_ima_name_old  TYPE pa0001-ename.

  CALL METHOD rem_sel_alv->check_changed_data.

  IF w_5100_refresh = abap_true.
    gt_outtab17_old2[] = gt_outtab17[].
    SORT gt_outtab17_old2.
    SORT gt_outtab17_old.
    IF gt_outtab17_old2[] NE gt_outtab17_old[].
      PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      IF w_exit IS INITIAL.
        w_ima             = w_ima_old.
        zim_screen-ass_to = w_ass_to_old.
        w_all_remind      = w_all_remind_old.
        w_ima_name        = w_ima_name_old.
        CLEAR w_5100_refresh.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR w_exit.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      gt_outtab17_old2[] = gt_outtab17[].
      SORT gt_outtab17_old2.
      SORT gt_outtab17_old.
      IF gt_outtab17_old2[] NE gt_outtab17_old[].
        PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
      IF w_exit = 'I'.
        PERFORM free_rem_sel.
        SET SCREEN 0.
        CLEAR w_code2.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.


  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'P--'.
      gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      CALL METHOD rem_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'P-'.
      CALL METHOD rem_sel_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      SUBTRACT 21 FROM gr_row_no-row_id.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD rem_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P+'.
      CALL METHOD rem_sel_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      ADD 21 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab17 LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 21.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD rem_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P++'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab17 LINES w_lines.
      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 20.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD rem_sel_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'UPD1' OR 'UPD3' OR 'UPDLEG' OR 'UPDDEP' OR
         'UPDCM' OR 'UPDIP'.
      CALL METHOD rem_sel_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab17_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab17_row_no.

      CALL METHOD rem_sel_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Case to be Processed'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Case'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab17 INTO gr_outtab17
                   INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        gr_outtab17_row_table = gr_row_table.
        w_5100_refresh = abap_true.
        IF w_code2 = 'UPD1'.
          w_2100_from   = 5100.
          PERFORM call_2100 USING gr_outtab17-pernr
                                  gr_outtab17-incident.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPD3'.
          w_3100_from   = 5100.
          PERFORM call_3100 USING gr_outtab17-pernr
                                  gr_outtab17-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPDLEG'.
          CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_LEG_REQ_DETAIL'
            EXPORTING
              i_action      = 'U'
              i_req_area    = 'L'
              i_pernr       = gr_outtab17-pernr
              i_incident    = gr_outtab17-incident
              i_seq_no      = 1
            EXCEPTIONS
              inc_not_found = 1
              OTHERS        = 2.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
            IF sy-subrc = 1.
              MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Legal Request does not exist'.
            ELSE.
              MESSAGE i999 WITH 'An error occurred accessing'
                                'the Legal Details'.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPDDEP'.
          w_4001_action = 'UPD'.
          w_4001_from   = 5100.
          PERFORM call_4001 USING gr_outtab17-pernr
                                  gr_outtab17-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPDCM'.
          w_3400_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3400_from   = 5100.
          PERFORM call_3400 USING gr_outtab17-pernr
                                  gr_outtab17-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'UPDIP'.
          w_3500_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3500_from   = 5100.
          PERFORM call_3500 USING gr_outtab17-pernr
                                  gr_outtab17-incident.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'SAVE'.
      IF w_error IS INITIAL.
        PERFORM save_rem_list.
        w_5100_refresh = abap_true.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Reminders/To Do List'
                          'has been successfully saved'.
        IF w_exit = abap_true.
          PERFORM free_rem_sel.
          SET SCREEN 0.
          CLEAR w_code2.
          LEAVE SCREEN.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  IF w_code2(4) = '%_GC' AND
     w_code2+9(2) = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_enter.
** Enter whilst in the ALV
** Unfortunately this doesn't work for change and enter at the same time
    PERFORM refresh_rem_sel_alv_total.
  ENDIF.



  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_5100  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_6000  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_6000 INPUT.

* This is required to update the current field when a function
* code is selected.
  CALL METHOD ltr_sel_alv->check_changed_data.


  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      PERFORM free_ltr_sel.
      w_canc = abap_true.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.

    WHEN 'ADDCORR'.
      CLEAR : zim_screen-corr_code, zim_ltr_deft, w_popup_ucomm.
      CALL SCREEN 7010 STARTING AT 45 14.
      IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
        PERFORM add_correspondence.
        PERFORM refresh_ltr_sel_alv_total.
      ENDIF.


    WHEN 'PROCLTR'.
      CLEAR w_error.
      PERFORM validate_correspondence TABLES gt_outtab14
                                      CHANGING w_error.
      IF w_error IS INITIAL.
        CLEAR : w_lines2.
        LOOP AT gt_outtab14 INTO gr_outtab14
          WHERE sel_code   IS NOT INITIAL.
          ADD 1 TO w_lines2.
        ENDLOOP.
        IF w_lines2 IS INITIAL.
          CLEAR w_code2.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'A Correspondence'
                            'must be selected'.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.
        PERFORM validate_insurer  TABLES gt_outtab14
                                  CHANGING w_error.
        IF w_error IS NOT INITIAL.
          MESSAGE w999  WITH 'Insurer should be selected for'
                             'one of the correspondence'.
        ENDIF.
        w_6100_from   = w_6000_from.
        PERFORM call_6100.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'NEXT'.

  ENDCASE.

* This is required to update the current field when a function
* code is selected.
  CALL METHOD ltr_sel_alv->check_changed_data.

  IF w_code2(4) = '%_GC'.
    IF w_code2+9(2) = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_enter.
* Enter whilst in the ALV
* Unfortunately this doesn't work for change and enter at the same time
*     perform validate_letter     tables gt_outtab11
*                                 changing w_error.
      PERFORM refresh_ltr_sel_alv_total.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_6000  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_6100  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_6100 INPUT.

* This is required to update the current field when a function
* code is selected.
  CALL METHOD ltr2_sel_alv->check_changed_data.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      PERFORM free_ltr_sel.
      PERFORM free_ltr2_sel.
      w_canc = abap_true.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.

    WHEN 'ADDREC'.
      CALL METHOD ltr2_sel_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab15_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab15_row_no.

      CALL METHOD ltr2_sel_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Correspondence for'
                              'the new Receiver'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Correspondence'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab15 INTO gr_outtab15 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        CLEAR : zim_screen-user_cat, zim_screen-user_code,
                zim_screen-user_name, zim_ltr_deft, w_popup_ucomm,
                zim_screen-email, zim_screen-fax,
                zim_screen-home, zim_screen-sms.
        zim_screen-corr_code = gr_outtab15-corr_code.
        zim_ltr_deft-corr_desc = gr_outtab15-corr_desc.
        CALL SCREEN 7020 STARTING AT 35 10.
        IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
          PERFORM add_receiver.
          PERFORM refresh_ltr2_sel_alv_total.
        ELSE.
          CLEAR w_canc.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'PROCLTR'.
      CLEAR w_error.
      PERFORM validate_letter     TABLES gt_outtab15
                                  CHANGING w_error.
      IF w_error IS INITIAL.
        CLEAR : w_lines.
        LOOP AT gt_outtab15 INTO gr_outtab15
          WHERE sel_code   IS NOT INITIAL.
          ADD 1 TO w_lines.
        ENDLOOP.
        IF w_lines IS INITIAL.
          CLEAR w_code2.
          MESSAGE e999 WITH 'A Correspondence'
                            'must be selected'.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.
        IF w_lines2 GT 0 OR
           w_lines3 GT 0.
          PERFORM confirm_email.
        ELSE.
          w_answer = '1'.
        ENDIF.

        IF w_answer = '1'.
          IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_SCU'.
            PERFORM call_7030_reminder.
          ENDIF.
          PERFORM process_letters     TABLES gt_outtab15
                                      CHANGING w_error.
          IF w_error IS INITIAL.
            PERFORM update_documents.
          ENDIF.
          IF w_resend = abap_true.
            MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Correspondence has been'
                              'successfully sent.'.
          ENDIF.
          PERFORM free_ltr_sel.
          PERFORM free_ltr2_sel.
          SET SCREEN 0.
          LEAVE SCREEN.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.


    WHEN 'NEXT'.

  ENDCASE.

* This is required to update the current field when a function
* code is selected.
  CALL METHOD ltr2_sel_alv->check_changed_data.

  IF w_code2(4) = '%_GC'.
    IF w_code2+9(2) = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_enter.
* Enter whilst in the ALV
* Unfortunately this doesn't work for change and enter at the same time
*     perform validate_letter     tables gt_outtab11
*                                 changing w_error.
      PERFORM refresh_ltr2_sel_alv_total.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR w_code2.



ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_6100  INPUT

  CASE w_popup_ucomm.
    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      w_canc = abap_true.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.

    WHEN 'OK'.
      CLEAR : w_popup_ucomm, w_canc.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.

ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_7010  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_7020  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_7020 INPUT.

  CASE w_popup_ucomm.
    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      w_canc = abap_true.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.

    WHEN 'RECTYPE'.
      CLEAR : zim_screen-user_code, zim_screen-user_name,
              zim_screen-fax, zim_screen-email,
              zim_screen-home, zim_screen-sms.
      IF zim_screen-user_cat = 'E'.
        zim_screen-user_code = zim_in_mast-pernr.
        zim_screen-user_name = w_emp_name.
        PERFORM get_emp_email USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                              CHANGING zim_screen-email.
        PERFORM get_emp_home_email USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                   CHANGING zim_screen-home
                                            zim_screen-sms.
      ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'C'.
        zim_screen-user_code = zim_in_mast-mgr.
        PERFORM get_sup_email USING zim_screen-user_code
                              CHANGING zim_screen-email
                                       zim_screen-user_name.
      ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'S'.
        zim_screen-user_code = zim_in_mast-sup.
        PERFORM get_sup_email USING zim_screen-user_code
                              CHANGING zim_screen-email
                                       zim_screen-user_name.
      ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'A'.
        zim_screen-user_code = zim_in_mast-sup_add.
        PERFORM get_sup_email USING zim_screen-user_code
                              CHANGING zim_screen-email
                                       zim_screen-user_name.
      ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'R'.
        zim_screen-user_code = zim_in_mast-lam.
        PERFORM get_sup_email USING zim_screen-user_code
                              CHANGING zim_screen-email
                                       zim_screen-user_name.
      ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'G'.
        zim_screen-user_code = zim_in_mast-gao.
        PERFORM get_sup_email USING zim_screen-user_code
                              CHANGING zim_screen-email
                                       zim_screen-user_name.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'OK'.
      IF zim_screen-corr_code IS INITIAL OR
         zim_screen-user_cat  IS INITIAL OR
         zim_screen-user_name IS INITIAL.
        CLEAR w_popup_ucomm.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Enter the Receivers Details'.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      LOOP AT gt_outtab15 INTO gr_outtab15
           WHERE corr_code = zim_screen-corr_code
             AND user_cat  = zim_screen-user_cat
             AND user_code = zim_screen-user_code
             AND user_name = zim_screen-user_name
             AND email     = zim_screen-email
             AND fax       = zim_screen-fax
             AND home      = zim_screen-home
             AND sms       = zim_screen-sms.
        CLEAR w_popup_ucomm.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'The Receiver is already selected'.
        EXIT.
      ENDLOOP.

      CLEAR : w_popup_ucomm, w_canc.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR : w_popup_ucomm.

ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_7020  INPUT
