*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_8080  INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_8080 INPUT.



  CASE w_popup_ucomm.
    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      w_canc = 'X'.
      PERFORM free_doc_link_txt .
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.

    WHEN 'OK'.

      CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS = 1.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      ENDIF.

      CALL METHOD doc_link_text->get_text_as_r3table
        IMPORTING
          table                  = gt_outtxt80
        EXCEPTIONS
          error_dp               = 1
          error_cntl_call_method = 2
          error_dp_create        = 3
          potential_data_loss    = 4.

      CALL METHOD doc_link_text->get_text_as_stream
        EXPORTING
          only_when_modified = 0
        IMPORTING
          text               = selected_text
        EXCEPTIONS
          error_dp           = 1.

      PERFORM free_doc_link_txt .
      CLEAR w_canc.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.

  ENDCASE.

  CALL METHOD doc_link_text->get_text_as_r3table
    IMPORTING
      table                  = gt_outtxt80
    EXCEPTIONS
      error_dp               = 1
      error_cntl_call_method = 2
      error_dp_create        = 3
      potential_data_loss    = 4.

  CLEAR : w_popup_ucomm.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_8080  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_8100  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_8100 INPUT.

  CASE w_popup_ucomm.
    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      w_canc = abap_true.
      PERFORM free_init_con.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.

    WHEN 'SAVE1'.
      CLEAR : w_popup_ucomm, w_canc.
      CALL METHOD init_con_alv->check_changed_data.
      PERFORM save_contact.
      IF w_error IS INITIAL.
        PERFORM free_init_con.
        SET SCREEN 0.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'SELALL'.
      LOOP AT gt_outtab5 INTO gr_outtab5.
        gr_outtab5-cont_act_comp = abap_true.
        MODIFY gt_outtab5 FROM gr_outtab5.
      ENDLOOP.
      CALL METHOD init_con_alv->refresh_table_display.

    WHEN 'DELALL'.
      LOOP AT gt_outtab5 INTO gr_outtab5.
        gr_outtab5-cont_act_comp = ' '.
        MODIFY gt_outtab5 FROM gr_outtab5.
      ENDLOOP.
      CALL METHOD init_con_alv->refresh_table_display.

    WHEN 'NEXT'.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR : w_popup_ucomm.

ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_8100  INPUT

        CLEAR w_ucomm.

      ENDIF.

      SET SCREEN 0000.
      LEAVE SCREEN.

  ENDCASE.


  CLEAR w_ucomm.



ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_8300  INPUT


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_9600  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_9600 INPUT.

  CALL METHOD doc_sel_alv->check_changed_data.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      PERFORM free_doc_sel.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.


    WHEN 'SAVE'.
      CLEAR w_error.
      PERFORM validate_doc_link   TABLES gt_outtab18
                                  CHANGING w_error.
      IF w_error IS INITIAL.
        PERFORM process_doc_link    TABLES gt_outtab18
                                    CHANGING w_error.
        PERFORM free_doc_sel.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Additional Files have been updated'.
        SET SCREEN 0.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.


    WHEN 'NEXT'.

  ENDCASE.

* This is required to update the current field when a function
* code is selected.
  CALL METHOD doc_sel_alv->check_changed_data.

  IF w_code2(4) = '%_GC'.
    IF w_code2+9(2) = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_enter.
* Enter whilst in the ALV
* Unfortunately this doesn't work for change and enter at the same time
      PERFORM validate_doc_link   TABLES gt_outtab18
                                  CHANGING w_error.
      PERFORM refresh_doc_sel_alv_total.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR w_code2.



ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_9600  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_CODE_HELP  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_code_help INPUT.

  IF zim_screen-user_cat = 'C' OR
     zim_screen-user_cat = 'S' OR
     zim_screen-user_cat = 'G' OR
     zim_screen-user_cat = 'R' OR
     zim_screen-user_cat = 'N'.
    PERFORM call_search_help USING    'ZZPREM'
                             CHANGING zim_screen-user_code.
  ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'I'.
    PERFORM f4_insurer_contact_2 USING zim_screen-user_code.
  ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'M' OR
         zim_screen-user_cat = 'L' OR
         zim_screen-user_cat = 'V' OR
         zim_screen-user_cat = 'D' OR
         zim_screen-user_cat = 'Y' OR
         zim_screen-user_cat = 'W'.
    PERFORM f4_advisor_help USING zim_screen-user_code.
  ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'H'.
    PERFORM call_search_help USING    'ZIMH_MED_PRAC'
                             CHANGING zim_screen-user_code.
  ELSEIF zim_screen-user_cat = 'J' OR
         zim_screen-user_cat = 'K' OR
         zim_screen-user_cat = 'P'.
    PERFORM call_search_help USING    'ZIMH_EXT_PROV'
                             CHANGING zim_screen-user_code.
  ENDIF.

ENDMODULE.                 " USER_CODE_HELP  INPUT

*&SPWIZARD: OUTPUT MODULE FOR TS 'incident'. DO NOT CHANGE THIS LINE!
*&SPWIZARD: SETS ACTIVE TAB
MODULE incident_active_tab_set OUTPUT.
  incident-activetab = g_incident-pressed_tab.
  CASE g_incident-pressed_tab.
    WHEN c_incident-tab1.
      g_incident-subscreen = '1001'.
    WHEN c_incident-tab2.
      g_incident-subscreen = '1002'.
    WHEN c_incident-tab3.
      g_incident-subscreen = '1003'.
    WHEN c_incident-tab4.
      g_incident-subscreen = '1004'.
    WHEN c_incident-tab5.
      g_incident-subscreen = '1005'.
    WHEN c_incident-tab6.
      g_incident-subscreen = '1006'.
    WHEN c_incident-tab7.
      g_incident-subscreen = '1007'.
    WHEN OTHERS.
*&SPWIZARD:      DO NOTHING
  ENDCASE.
ENDMODULE.                    "incident_ACTIVE_TAB_SET OUTPUT

*&SPWIZARD: INPUT MODULE FOR TS 'incident'. DO NOT CHANGE THIS LINE!
*&SPWIZARD: GETS ACTIVE TAB
MODULE incident_active_tab_get INPUT.
  w_code2 = sy-ucomm.
  CASE w_code2.
    WHEN c_incident-tab1.
      g_incident-pressed_tab = c_incident-tab1.
    WHEN c_incident-tab2.
      g_incident-pressed_tab = c_incident-tab2.
    WHEN c_incident-tab3.
      g_incident-pressed_tab = c_incident-tab3.
    WHEN c_incident-tab4.
      g_incident-pressed_tab = c_incident-tab4.
    WHEN c_incident-tab5.
      g_incident-pressed_tab = c_incident-tab5.
    WHEN c_incident-tab6.
      g_incident-pressed_tab = c_incident-tab6.
    WHEN c_incident-tab7.
      g_incident-pressed_tab = c_incident-tab7.
    WHEN OTHERS.
*&SPWIZARD:      DO NOTHING
  ENDCASE.
ENDMODULE.                    "incident_ACTIVE_TAB_GET INPUT

  CASE w_code2.
    WHEN c_init_scu-tab1.
      g_init_scu-pressed_tab = c_init_scu-tab1.
    WHEN c_init_scu-tab2.
      g_init_scu-pressed_tab = c_init_scu-tab2.
    WHEN c_init_scu-tab3.
      g_init_scu-pressed_tab = c_init_scu-tab3.
    WHEN c_init_scu-tab4.
      g_init_scu-pressed_tab = c_init_scu-tab4.
    WHEN c_init_scu-tab5.
      g_init_scu-pressed_tab = c_init_scu-tab5.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_3310  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_3310 OUTPUT.

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF depref_sel_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT depref_sel_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'DEP_CREA_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT depref_sel_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = depref_sel_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .

    "Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_button_click_3310
                FOR depref_sel_alv.


    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

    "provide a title
*    depref_sel_layout-grid_title =
*           'Select the existing deployment cases'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    depref_sel_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    depref_sel_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    depref_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    depref_sel_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    depref_sel_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_deploy_sel TABLES depref_sel_fieldcat.

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES depref_sel_functions.

    REFRESH depref_sel_style.
    CLEAR   depref_sel_style.

    CALL METHOD depref_sel_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = depref_sel_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = depref_sel_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = depref_sel_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab40.

    w_3310_refresh = abap_true.

  ENDIF.

  IF w_3310_refresh = abap_true.
    PERFORM get_depref.

    CLEAR w_3310_refresh.
    CALL METHOD depref_sel_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = depref_sel_layout.
    depref_sel_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    CALL METHOD depref_sel_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = depref_sel_layout.

    CALL METHOD depref_sel_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_3310  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SCREEN_CONTROL_3310  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_3310 OUTPUT.

  DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab40 LINES w_lines.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF w_3300_action = 'DISP'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ELSE.
      IF screen-name = 'DEP_CREA' AND w_lines GT 0.
        screen-input  = '0'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_3310  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3310  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3310 INPUT.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'DEP_A'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab40 LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines GT 0.
        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'A Referral to Deployment'
                          'has already been created'.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM maintain_depref  USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                       zim_in_mast-incident
                                       'I'.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3310  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_4000  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_4000 OUTPUT.

  REFRESH: exclfunc.
  CLEAR: exclfunc.

  SET TITLEBAR  '4000D'.

* Start oof CR3197  DEVK9HRFUU
  IF sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGRDEP'.   "CR3197  DEVK9HRFUU
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'EDITNOTES'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'DELDOC'.
  ENDIF.
* End of CR3197  DEVK9HRFUU

  SET PF-STATUS '4000D' EXCLUDING exclfunc.

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF depref_lst_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT depref_lst_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'DEPREF_LST_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT depref_lst_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = depref_lst_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .

    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

    "provide a title
*    rejloc_sel_layout-grid_title =
*           'Select the applicants to be processed'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
*   INIT_ASS_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    depref_lst_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    depref_lst_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    depref_lst_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    depref_lst_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_depref_lst TABLES depref_lst_fieldcat.

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES depref_lst_functions.

    REFRESH depref_lst_style.
    CLEAR   depref_lst_style.

    PERFORM get_dep_lst.

    CALL METHOD depref_lst_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = depref_lst_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = depref_lst_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = depref_lst_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab40d.

    CLEAR w_4000_refresh.

  ENDIF.

  IF w_4000_refresh = abap_true.
    PERFORM get_dep_lst.

    CLEAR w_4000_refresh.
    CALL METHOD depref_lst_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = depref_lst_layout.
    depref_lst_layout-cwidth_opt = abap_true.
    CALL METHOD depref_lst_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = depref_lst_layout.
    CALL METHOD depref_lst_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_4000  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  CHECK_SCREEN_4000  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE check_screen_4000 INPUT.

  IF w_code2 = 'BACK' OR
     w_code2 = 'CANC' OR
     w_code2 = 'EXIT'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM validate_4000.

  w_4000_refresh = abap_true.

ENDMODULE.                 " CHECK_SCREEN_4000  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_4000  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_4000 INPUT.
  DATA: l_ok1_4001    TYPE c.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      PERFORM free_depref_lst.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      CLEAR w_code2.
      LEAVE SCREEN.


    WHEN 'P--'.
      gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      CALL METHOD depref_lst_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'P-'.
      CALL METHOD depref_lst_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      SUBTRACT 22 FROM gr_row_no-row_id.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD depref_lst_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P+'.
      CALL METHOD depref_lst_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_row_no.
      ADD 22 TO gr_row_no-row_id.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab40d LINES w_lines.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id > w_lines.
        gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 22.
      ENDIF.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD depref_lst_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.

    WHEN 'P++'.
      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtab40d LINES w_lines.
      gr_row_no-row_id = w_lines - 21.
      IF gr_row_no-row_id < 1.
        gr_row_no-row_id = 1.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD depref_lst_alv->set_scroll_info_via_id
        EXPORTING
          is_col_info = gr_col_info
          is_row_no   = gr_row_no.


    WHEN 'DISP1' OR 'DISP2' OR 'DISP3' OR 'DISPDOC' OR 'DISPLEG' OR
         'DEPREF' OR 'DEPACT' OR 'CLOSE' OR 'DISP6' OR 'DISP7'   OR
         'EDITNOTES' OR 'DELDOC'.  "CR3197  DEVK9HRFUU

      CALL METHOD depref_lst_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab40d_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab40d_row_no.

      CALL METHOD depref_lst_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Referral to be Processed'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one Referral'.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab40d
          INTO gr_outtab40d INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        gr_outtab40d_row_table = gr_row_table.
        w_4000_refresh = abap_true.

        IF w_code2 = 'DISP1'.
          w_1000_action = 'DISP'.
          w_1000_from   = 4000.
          PERFORM call_1000_a USING gr_outtab40d-pernr
                                    gr_outtab40d-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP2'.
          w_2100_action = 'DISP'.
          w_2100_from   = 4000.
          PERFORM call_2100 USING gr_outtab40d-pernr
                                  gr_outtab40d-incident.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISPDOC'.
          CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
            EXPORTING
              i_action   = 'U'
              i_pernr    = gr_outtab40d-pernr
              i_incident = gr_outtab40d-incident.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISPLEG'.
          w_3200_action = 'DISP'.
          w_3200_from   = 4000.
          PERFORM call_3200 USING gr_outtab40d-pernr
                                  gr_outtab40d-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DEPACT'.
          w_4001_action = 'UPD'.
          w_4001_from   = 4000.
          PERFORM call_4001 USING gr_outtab40d-pernr
                                  gr_outtab40d-incident.

        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'CLOSE'.
          w_3300_action = 'UPD'.
          w_3300_from   = 4000.
          PERFORM call_3300 USING gr_outtab40d-pernr
                                  gr_outtab40d-incident.


        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP6'.
          w_4001_action = 'DISP'.
          w_4001_from   = 4000.
          PERFORM call_4001 USING gr_outtab40d-pernr
                                  gr_outtab40d-incident.


        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DISP7'.
          w_4000_action = 'DISP'.
          w_4000_from   = 4000.
          PERFORM maintain_depref  USING gr_outtab40d-pernr
                                         gr_outtab40d-incident
                                         'D'.
* Start of CR3197  DEVK9HRFUU
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'EDITNOTES'.
          w_3011_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_3011 USING gr_outtab40d-pernr
                                  gr_outtab40d-incident.
        ELSEIF w_code2 = 'DELDOC'.
          w_3012_from   = 3010.
          PERFORM call_3012 USING gr_outtab40d-pernr
                                  gr_outtab40d-incident.
* End of CR3197  DEVK9HRFUU

        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_4000  INPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SCREEN_CONTROL_4002  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_4002 OUTPUT.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    IF w_4001_action = 'DISP'.
*   Set all fields to Display Only
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ELSE.
*   Set the Restricted field to update for Managers only
      IF screen-group1 = '100'.
        IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRDEP' OR
           sy-tcode = 'ZIM_DEP'    OR
           sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGR'.
          screen-input  = '1'.
        ELSE.
          screen-input  = '0'.
        ENDIF.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.
ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_4002  OUTPUT

*          USING zim_in_mast-pernr

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_4004  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_4004 OUTPUT.

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF depref1_txt_text IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT depref1_txt_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name = 'DEPREF1_TXT_CONT'.

    CREATE OBJECT depref1_txt_text
      EXPORTING
        parent                     = depref1_txt_container
        wordwrap_mode              = 2
        wordwrap_position          = 100
        wordwrap_to_linebreak_mode = 0.

    "Hide the toolbar
    CALL METHOD depref1_txt_text->set_toolbar_mode
      EXPORTING
        toolbar_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>false.

    "Hide the status bar
    CALL METHOD depref1_txt_text->set_statusbar_mode
      EXPORTING
        statusbar_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>false.

    "Hide the toolbar
    CALL METHOD depref1_txt_text->set_local_contextmenu_mode
      EXPORTING
        visible = cl_gui_textedit=>true.


    CALL METHOD depref1_txt_text->set_enable
      EXPORTING
        enable            = cl_gui_cfw=>true
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error        = 1
        cntl_system_error = 2
        OTHERS            = 3.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_4001_action = 'DISP' OR
       zim_in_deploy-deploy_status = 'C' OR
       w_readonly    = abap_true    OR
       ( sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_DEP' AND
         sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGRDEP' ).

      CALL METHOD depref1_txt_text->set_readonly_mode
        EXPORTING
          readonly_mode          = 1
        EXCEPTIONS
          error_cntl_call_method = 1
          invalid_parameter      = 2
          OTHERS                 = 3.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    CALL METHOD depref1_txt_text->set_text_as_r3table
      EXPORTING
        table           = gt_outtxt10_dep
      EXCEPTIONS
        error_dp        = 1
        error_dp_create = 2
        OTHERS          = 3.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.
      IF zim_in_deploy-deploy_status NE 'C'.

        DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtxt10_dep LINES w_lines.

        CALL METHOD depref1_txt_text->go_to_line
          EXPORTING
            line                   = w_lines
          EXCEPTIONS
            error_cntl_call_method = 1
            OTHERS                 = 2.

        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        ENDIF.

        SUBTRACT 1 FROM w_lines.
        IF w_lines GT 0.
          CALL METHOD depref1_txt_text->protect_lines
            EXPORTING
              from_line              = 1
              to_line                = w_lines
            EXCEPTIONS
              error_cntl_call_method = 1
              invalid_parameter      = 2
              OTHERS                 = 3.

          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

  w_doc_link = 'D'.

ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_4404  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  STATUS_4001  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE status_4001 OUTPUT.
  DATA: l_action_desc(10) TYPE c.

  REFRESH: exclfunc.
  CLEAR: exclfunc.

  IF w_4001_action = 'DISP'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'SAVE'.
    PERFORM exclfunc_append USING 'IMPORT'.
    l_action_desc = 'Display'.
  ELSE.
    l_action_desc = 'Update'.
  ENDIF.

  SET TITLEBAR  '4001' WITH l_action_desc.
* Start of CR3197  DEVK9HRFUU
  gv_btn_dyn_txt-icon_id   = '@DK@'.  "ICON_WORD_PROCESSING
  gv_btn_dyn_txt-icon_text = 'Deploy Case Notes'.
  gv_btn_dyn_txt-quickinfo = 'Deploy Case Notes'.
  gv_btn_dyn_txt-text      = 'Notes1'.
  gv_btn_dyn_txt-path      = ' '.
* End of CR3197  DEVK9HRFUU
  SET PF-STATUS '4001' EXCLUDING exclfunc.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_4001  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_4001  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_4001 INPUT.

*  CLEAR w_call_2100.

  PERFORM check_text_status_dep_act.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      IF w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.

        IF w_4001_changed IS NOT INITIAL.
          PERFORM back_scrn_popup.
        ELSE.
          w_exit = 'I'.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        w_exit = 'I'.
      ENDIF.

      IF w_exit = 'I'.

        PERFORM exit_4001.

        CLEAR : zim_screen_depext, zim_screen_depmtg,
                zim_screen_deprev, w_orgeh_name,
                w_emp_plans, w_emp_plans_txt, w_emp_org,
                w_emp_org_txt.

        REFRESH: gt_outtxt10_dep, gt_outtxt2a, gt_outtxt2b,
                  gt_outtxt10, gt_outtxt11, gt_outtxt2c.


        CLEAR : w_code2, w_4001_action, w_old_depstat.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CASE w_code2.

    WHEN 'NOTES1'.
      IF w_4001_action = 'DISP'.
        w_call_act = abap_true.
      ELSE.
        w_call_act = ' '.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                               zim_in_mast-incident
                               'DEPLOYMENT'
                               ' '
                               w_call_act.

    WHEN 'NOTES3'.
      w_call_act = abap_true.
      PERFORM call_notes USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                               zim_in_mast-incident
                               'CASE'
                               ' '
                               w_call_act.

    WHEN 'NOTESALL1'.
      SUBMIT zhrim_notes_overview_dep
             WITH p_pernr = zim_in_mast-pernr
             WITH p_inc   = zim_in_mast-incident
             WITH p_rest  = abap_true
             WITH r_case  = abap_true
             WITH r_mgrc  = abap_true
             WITH r_empc  = abap_true
             WITH r_meetd = abap_true
             WITH r_meetf = abap_true
             WITH r_meeto = abap_true
             WITH r_tp    = abap_true
      AND RETURN.

    WHEN 'IMPORT'.
      CLEAR : zim_screen-ltr_category, w_canc.
      REFRESH r_cat.
      PERFORM add_r_cat USING '18'.
      PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'R1'.

      CALL SCREEN 8300 STARTING AT 45 10.

      IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
        CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_IMPORT_DOCUMENT'
          EXPORTING
            i_pernr       = zim_in_mast-pernr
            i_incident    = zim_in_mast-incident
            i_doc_unique  = 'DEP001'
            i_cat         = zim_screen-ltr_category
            i_direction   = 'I'
          IMPORTING
            o_arch_object = l_obj.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'DISP2'.
      w_2100_from   = 4001.
      w_2100_action = 'DISP'.
      PERFORM call_2100_a USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                zim_in_mast-incident.

    WHEN 'DISPDOC'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_DOCUMENT_OVERVIEW'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_3100_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'SAVE'.
*     Validate 4004
      PERFORM validate_4002.
*     Validate 4005
*     PERFORM validate_4005.
*     Validate 4007
*     PERFORM validate_4007.

      PERFORM save_zim_in_deploy.

      PERFORM save_depref1_notes.    "4004
      PERFORM save_deployment_notes. "4005
      PERFORM save_dep_mtg.          "4005
      PERFORM save_dep_review.       "4005
      PERFORM save_dep_placement.    "4006
      PERFORM save_dep_external.     "4007

      PERFORM save_im_mast_deploy.

      PERFORM set_text_deploy_activities.

*      w_4004_refresh = abap_true.
      w_4005_refresh = abap_true.
      w_4006_refresh = abap_true.
*     w_4007_refresh = abap_true.

      CLEAR : zim_screen_depext,
              w_orgeh_name.
      REFRESH: gt_outtxt10_dep, gt_outtxt2a, gt_outtxt2b,
                gt_outtxt10, gt_outtxt11, gt_outtxt2c.
      MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Case has been successfully saved'.

      IF w_exit = abap_true.
        PERFORM exit_4001.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR w_4001_changed.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'P902'.
      PERFORM display_p902 USING 'O'.

    WHEN 'REM'.
      PERFORM call_7030_reminder.

    WHEN 'DISPREM'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_REMINDER_OVERVIEW'
        STARTING NEW TASK 'DISPREM'
        DESTINATION 'NONE'
        EXPORTING
          i_action   = w_4001_action(1)
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_tcode    = sy-tcode.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

  CLEAR w_code2.


ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_4001  INPUT

  DATA: l_succ  TYPE c.

*      ENDIF.

*      w_4005_refresh = abap_true.
*
*
*    WHEN 'MTG_CONF'.

*         zim_in_deploy-first_mtg_tm IS INITIAL.
*        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the Meeting Date '
*                          'and Meeting Time prior to sending '

*      ELSE.

*        PERFORM save_dep_mtg.
*
*        DESCRIBE TABLE  gt_outtab44 LINES w_lines.
*        REFRESH r_cat.
*        IF w_lines = 1.
*          PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'R5'.

*        ELSEIF w_lines > 1.

*                                       abap_true.
*
*        ENDIF.
*
*
*        IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
*          WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.
**       Save entries of zim_in_deploy
*          zim_in_deploy-mtg_corr_sent_dt = sy-datum.
*          PERFORM save_zim_in_deploy.
**          MESSAGE i999 WITH 'Command and Employee has been '
**                       'informed of Deployment Initial Meeting.'.
*
**       Send Reminder to IMA of the email for the dtls of the
**       initial/succeeding meeting
*          PERFORM create_reminder USING 'I'
*                                    zim_in_mast-ima
*                                    '20'
*                                    '05'
*                                    ' '
*                                    '00000000'.
*        ENDIF.
*      ENDIF.
*
*      w_4005_refresh = abap_true.
*
*
*    WHEN 'AGR_GOAL'.
*      IF NOT goal_txt_container IS INITIAL.
*        CALL METHOD goal_txt_text->get_text_as_r3table
*          IMPORTING
*            table  = gt_outtxt2b
*          EXCEPTIONS
*            OTHERS = 1.
*

**          EXCEPTIONS
**            OTHERS = 1.

**          EXCEPTIONS
**            error_cntl_call_method = 1
**            OTHERS                 = 2.
*      ENDIF.
*
*      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtxt2b LINES w_lines.
*      IF w_lines = 0.
*        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Agreed Goals have to be entered'
*                          'before they can be sent'.
*      ELSE.
*        w_goal_key = 'DGOAL'.
*        PERFORM save_text_contact TABLES gt_outtxt2b
*                                         gt_outtxt2b_old
*                                  USING  w_goal_key
*                                         '001'
*                                         ' '.
*        CONCATENATE 'DGOAL' '001' INTO w_doc_unique.
**      rec_zim_in_rehab = zim_in_rehab.
*        REFRESH r_cat.
*        PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'R6'.
*        PERFORM correspondence USING zim_in_mast-pernr
*                                    zim_in_mast-incident
*                                    w_doc_unique
*                                    abap_true.
*        IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
*          WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.
*
*          zim_in_deploy-goals_sent_dt = sy-datum.
**     Save entries of zim_in_deploy
*          PERFORM save_zim_in_deploy.
**        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Agreed Goals has been'
**                          'sent to the Command and Employee.'.
*
**     Send Reminder to IMA of the email for the dtls of the
**     agreed goals
*          PERFORM create_reminder USING 'I'
*                                      zim_in_mast-ima
*                                      '20'
*                                      '06'
*                                      ' '
*                                      '00000000'.
*
*        ENDIF.
*        w_4005_refresh = abap_true.
*      ENDIF.
*
*    WHEN 'CASE_CONF'.
*      IF zim_in_deploy-mth_case_rev_dt IS INITIAL OR
*         zim_in_deploy-mth_case_rev_tm IS INITIAL.
*        MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Please enter the Case Review Date '
*                          'and Case Review Time prior to sending '
*                          'the confirmation'.
*      ELSE.
*        PERFORM save_zim_in_deploy.
*        PERFORM save_dep_review.
*
*        REFRESH r_cat.
*        PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'RN'.
*        PERFORM correspondence USING zim_in_mast-pernr
*                                     zim_in_mast-incident
*                                    'DEPREF6'
*                                    abap_true.
*        IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
*          WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.
**       Save entries of zim_in_deploy
*          zim_in_deploy-mth_crev_sent_dt = sy-datum.
*          PERFORM save_zim_in_deploy.
**          MESSAGE i999 WITH 'Command and Employee has been '
**                       'informed of Deployment Initial Meeting.'.
*
**       Send Reminder to IMA of the email for the dtls of the
**       initial meeting
*          PERFORM create_reminder USING 'I'
*                                    zim_in_mast-ima
*                                    '20'
*                                    '32'
*                                    ' '
*                                    '00000000'.
*        ENDIF.
*      ENDIF.
*
*      w_4005_refresh = abap_true.
*
*    WHEN OTHERS.
*      "Do Nothing
*
*  ENDCASE.

ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_4005  INPUT

MODULE status_4006 OUTPUT.

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF prop_pos_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT prop_pos_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'PROP_POS_CONT'
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error                  = 1
        cntl_system_error           = 2
        create_error                = 3
        lifetime_error              = 4
        lifetime_dynpro_dynpro_link = 5.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.

    "Create alv control
    CREATE OBJECT prop_pos_alv
      EXPORTING
        i_parent      = prop_pos_container
        i_appl_events = abap_true.

    "Creating an instance for the event handler
    CREATE OBJECT gr_event_handler .

    "Registering handler methods to handle ALV Grid events
    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_button_click_4006
                FOR prop_pos_alv.
    "Creating an instance for ALV changes
    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

    "provide a title
*    rejloc_sel_layout-grid_title =
*           'Select the applicants to be processed'.

    "inform ALV about field for setting a specific color
    prop_pos_layout-no_toolbar = abap_true.
    prop_pos_layout-sel_mode   = 'A'.
    prop_pos_layout-cwidth_opt = ' '.
    prop_pos_layout-stylefname = 'CELLSTYLES'.
    prop_pos_layout-info_fname = 'ROW_COLOUR'.

    PERFORM build_fieldcat_prop_pos TABLES prop_pos_fieldcat.

    PERFORM exclude_functions_ind TABLES prop_pos_functions.

    CALL METHOD prop_pos_alv->register_edit_event
      EXPORTING
        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_modified.

    CALL METHOD prop_pos_alv->register_edit_event
      EXPORTING
        i_event_id = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_evt_enter.

    CALL METHOD prop_pos_alv->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = prop_pos_layout
        it_toolbar_excluding = prop_pos_functions
      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = prop_pos_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab41.

    w_4006_refresh = 'X'.

  ENDIF.



  IF w_4006_refresh = abap_true.
    PERFORM get_proposed_pos.

    CLEAR w_4006_refresh.
    CALL METHOD prop_pos_alv->get_frontend_layout
      IMPORTING
        es_layout = prop_pos_layout.
    prop_pos_layout-cwidth_opt = ' '.

    CALL METHOD prop_pos_alv->set_frontend_layout
      EXPORTING
        is_layout = prop_pos_layout.

    "Set the layout depending on the Placement Type
    PERFORM build_fieldcat_prop_pos TABLES prop_pos_fieldcat.
    CALL METHOD prop_pos_alv->set_frontend_fieldcatalog
      EXPORTING
        it_fieldcatalog = prop_pos_fieldcat.

    CALL METHOD prop_pos_alv->refresh_table_display.
  ENDIF.

ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_4006  OUTPUT

MODULE user_command_4006 INPUT.
  DATA: l_ok      TYPE c,
        l_succ2   TYPE c,
        l_cont    TYPE c.

* Update the table with any changes
  CALL METHOD prop_pos_alv->check_changed_data.
* Validations that use data from an ALV table can only be performed
* in this step, they do not work in the Chain step

  CLEAR w_canc.

  CASE w_code2.
      CLEAR: l_succ2.

    WHEN 'TP_INS'.
      PERFORM maintain_tp
        USING 'I'
              w_next_pos_seq_no.

    WHEN OTHERS.
      "Do Nothing

  ENDCASE.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_4020  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_4020 INPUT.
  CASE w_ucomm.

    WHEN 'CANC' OR 'BACK' OR 'EXIT'.
      w_ret_ucomm = w_ucomm.
      PERFORM free_wcert_sel.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.

    WHEN 'EXEC'.
      CALL METHOD wcert_sel_alv->get_scroll_info_via_id
        IMPORTING
          es_col_info = gr_outtab42_col_info
          es_row_no   = gr_outtab42_row_no.

      CALL METHOD wcert_sel_alv->get_selected_rows
        IMPORTING
          et_index_rows = gt_row_table
          et_row_no     = gt_row_no
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE a000.
      ENDIF.

      DESCRIBE TABLE gt_row_table LINES w_lines.
      IF w_lines = 0.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Select a Certificate '
                              'for the incident.'.
      ELSEIF w_lines GT 1.
        MESSAGE i999(zz) WITH 'Only select one certificate.'.
      ELSE.
        w_ret_ucomm = w_ucomm.
        READ TABLE gt_row_table INTO gr_row_table INDEX 1.
        READ TABLE gt_outtab42
          INTO gr_outtab42 INDEX gr_row_table-index.
        PERFORM insert_9203.
        PERFORM edit_9203
          USING gr_outtab42-pernr
                gr_outtab42-date_from '99991231'.

        PERFORM free_wcert_sel.

        SET SCREEN 0.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.
ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_4020  INPUT

  "Instantiate the variables CONTAINER and ALV
  IF depext_req_alv IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT depext_req_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name              = 'DEPEXT_REQ_CONT'

    SET HANDLER gr_event_handler->handle_data_changed_4007
                FOR depext_req_alv.

    CREATE OBJECT gt_table_changed.

    "provide a title

      EXPORTING

      CHANGING
        it_fieldcatalog      = depext_req_fieldcat
        it_outtab            = gt_outtab43.

    w_4007_refresh = abap_true.

  ENDIF.

  IF depext_txt_text IS INITIAL.

    CREATE OBJECT depext_txt_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name = 'DEPEXT_TXT_CONT'.

    CREATE OBJECT depext_txt_text
      EXPORTING
        parent                     = depext_txt_container
        wordwrap_mode              = 2
        wordwrap_position          = 100
        wordwrap_to_linebreak_mode = 0.


    "Hide the toolbar
    CALL METHOD depext_txt_text->set_toolbar_mode
      EXPORTING
        toolbar_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>false.

      EXPORTING

      EXPORTING
        enable            = cl_gui_cfw=>true
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error        = 1
        cntl_system_error = 2
        OTHERS            = 3.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.

      EXPORTING
        table           = gt_outtxt11
      EXCEPTIONS

        error_dp_create = 2
        OTHERS          = 3.

    ENDIF.

    CALL METHOD depext_txt_text->go_to_line
      EXPORTING
        line                   = w_lines
      EXCEPTIONS
        error_cntl_call_method = 1
        OTHERS                 = 2.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    ENDIF.

    SUBTRACT 1 FROM w_lines.
    IF w_lines GT 0.
      CALL METHOD depext_txt_text->protect_lines
        EXPORTING
          from_line              = 1
          to_line                = w_lines
        EXCEPTIONS
          error_cntl_call_method = 1
          invalid_parameter      = 2
          OTHERS                 = 3.

      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

        enable            = cl_gui_cfw=>true
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_error        = 1
        cntl_system_error = 2
        OTHERS            = 3.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_4001_action = 'DISP' OR
       zim_in_deploy-deploy_status = 'C' OR
       w_readonly    = abap_true    OR
       ( sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_DEP' AND
         sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGRDEP' ).
      CALL METHOD mgr_txt1_text->set_readonly_mode
        EXPORTING
          readonly_mode          = 1
        EXCEPTIONS
          error_cntl_call_method = 1
          invalid_parameter      = 2
          OTHERS                 = 3.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    CALL METHOD mgr_txt1_text->set_text_as_r3table
      EXPORTING
        table           = gt_outtxt2c
      EXCEPTIONS
        error_dp        = 1
        error_dp_create = 2
        OTHERS          = 3.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_4001_action NE 'DISP'.
      IF zim_in_deploy-deploy_status NE 'C'.
        DESCRIBE TABLE gt_outtxt2c LINES w_lines.

        CALL METHOD mgr_txt1_text->go_to_line
          EXPORTING
            line                   = w_lines
          EXCEPTIONS
            error_cntl_call_method = 1
            OTHERS                 = 2.

        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        ENDIF.

        SUBTRACT 1 FROM w_lines.
        IF w_lines GT 0.
          CALL METHOD mgr_txt1_text->protect_lines
            EXPORTING
              from_line              = 1
              to_line                = w_lines
            EXCEPTIONS
              error_cntl_call_method = 1
              invalid_parameter      = 2
              OTHERS                 = 3.

          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

*    CALL METHOD emp_txt1_text->set_text_as_stream
*      EXPORTING
*        text = gt_outtxt2a
*      EXCEPTIONS
**   error_dp =1
*        error_dp_create = 2.
*
  ENDIF.


ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_4013  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SCREEN_CONTROL_3311  OUTPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_3311 OUTPUT.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF w_3300_action = 'DISP' OR
       w_4001_action = 'DISP' OR
       zim_in_deploy-pernr IS INITIAL.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.

    ELSE.
*      IF screen-group1 = '100'.
*        IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRDEP' OR
*           sy-tcode = 'ZIM_SIMA'   OR
*           sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGR'.
*          screen-input  = '1'.
*        ELSE.
*          screen-input  = '0'.
*        ENDIF.
*        MODIFY SCREEN.
*      ENDIF.

      IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_DEP' OR
         sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRDEP'.
        IF screen-group2 = '020' OR
           screen-group2 = '030'.
          screen-input  = '0'.
        ELSEIF screen-group2 = '010'.
          screen-input  = '1'.
        ENDIF.
        MODIFY SCREEN.

      ELSEIF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_IMA'.
        IF screen-group2 = '020'.
          screen-input  = '1'.
        ELSEIF screen-group2 = '010' OR
               screen-group2 = '030'.
          screen-input  = '0'.
        ENDIF.
        MODIFY SCREEN.

      ELSEIF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_SIMA'.
        IF screen-group2 = '020' OR
           screen-group2 = '030'.
          screen-input  = '1'.
        ELSEIF screen-group2 = '010'.
          screen-input  = '0'.
        ENDIF.
        MODIFY SCREEN.

      ENDIF.


    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_3311  OUTPUT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3311  INPUT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE user_command_3311 INPUT.
  DATA: l_cont4 TYPE c.

  CLEAR: l_cont4, w_canc.

  CASE w_code2.

*    IF zim_in_deploy-deploy_status NE 'M'.
*      MESSAGE i999
*        WITH 'Deployment Status must be '
*             'Medical Discharge'.
*      l_cont4 = 'N'.
*    ELSE.
*      CLEAR: l_cont4.
*    ENDIF.
*
*    CHECK l_cont4 IS INITIAL.

      IF zim_in_deploy-pernr IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM save_zim_in_deploy_3300.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'CLOSERPT1'.
*      PERFORM maintain_document USING 'ZB'
*                                      'CLOSE'.
      PERFORM get_depmsg USING zim_in_mast-pernr.

      IF w_4001_msg = 'Post 88 Officer'.
        PERFORM maintain_document USING 'Z3'
                                     'CLOSE'.
      ELSEIF w_4001_msg = 'Civilian Employee'.
*        PERFORM maintain_document USING 'Z4'
        PERFORM maintain_document USING 'Z3'

                                     'CLOSE'.
      ENDIF.


    WHEN 'CLOSERPT2'.
      CONCATENATE 'CLOSE' '001' INTO w_doc_unique.
      PERFORM correspondence_send USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                        zim_in_mast-incident
                                        w_doc_unique
                                        ' '.
*      IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
*        zim_in_mast-closure_status = 'M'.
*        zim_in_deploy-deploy_status  = 'C'.
*        zim_in_mast-date_closed    = sy-datum.
*        PERFORM save_im_mast.
*
*        zim_in_deploy-date_closed = sy-datum.
**       Save zim_in_deploy
*        PERFORM SAVE_ZIM_IN_DEPLOY_3300.
*
*      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'DEPREP1'.
*      PERFORM save_zim_in_deploy_3300.

      PERFORM maintain_document USING 'RG'
                                      'DEPREP'.

    WHEN 'NOT_IMA'.
      "Send Reminder to IMA that the Dep Unit Rep
*   has been completed
      PERFORM create_reminder USING 'I'
                                    zim_in_mast-ima
                                    '20'
                                    '09'
                                    ' '
                                    '00000000'.

      zim_in_deploy-deploy_status  = 'M'.
      PERFORM save_im_mast.
      MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The IMA has been notified'
                        'of the Report'.


    WHEN 'SEND_CMND'.
      CLEAR: w_canc.
      REFRESH r_cat.
      IF w_persk GE 'XA' AND w_persk LE 'XF'.
        PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'RH'.
      ELSEIF  ( w_persk GE 'XG' AND w_persk LE 'XT' ) OR
                w_persk = 'XX' OR
* CR3185 DEVK9HRFNJ - MD
                w_persk = 'XY'.
        PERFORM add_r_cat USING 'RS'.
      ENDIF.

      w_doc_unique = 'CLOSECMD'.

      PERFORM correspondence USING zim_in_mast-pernr
                                   zim_in_mast-incident
                                   w_doc_unique
                                   abap_true.
      IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
        WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.
        zim_in_deploy-comd_rep_sent_dt = sy-datum.
        PERFORM save_zim_in_deploy_3300.

        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The Command Template has been'
                          'sent to the Command'.

      ENDIF.


    WHEN 'UPLD_CMD'.
      PERFORM upload_doc USING 'MEDDISCMD'
                               ' '
                               'RI'.
      IF w_canc IS INITIAL.
        WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.
        zim_in_deploy-comd_rep_recd_dt = sy-datum.
        PERFORM save_zim_in_deploy_3300.
        MESSAGE i999
          WITH 'Successful upload of the Completed'
               'Command Report.'.

      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'IMA_REP'.
      REFRESH r_cat.
      IF w_persk GE 'XA' AND w_persk LE 'XF'.
        PERFORM maintain_document USING 'RJ'
                                      'IMAREP'.
      ELSEIF ( w_persk GE 'XG' AND w_persk LE 'XT' ) OR
               w_persk = 'XX'.
        PERFORM maintain_document USING 'RT'
                                      'IMAREP'.

      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'NOT_FTCTL'.
      "Send Reminder to FTCL that the employee has been
      "referred to FTC
      IF zim_in_deploy-sima_cnfrm IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM create_reminder USING 'T'
                                    ' '
                                    '55'
                                    '08'
                                    ' '
                                    '00000000'.

        "Send Reminder to IMA that the employee has been
        "referred to FTC
        PERFORM create_reminder USING 'I'
                                    zim_in_mast-ima
                                    '20'
                                    '33'
                                    ' '
                                    '00000000'.

        WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.
        zim_in_mast-closure_status = 'M'.
*         zim_in_deploy-deploy_status  = 'M'.
*         zim_in_mast-date_closed    = sy-datum.
        zim_in_mast-inc_status     = 'F'.
        PERFORM save_im_mast.

        WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.
        zim_in_deploy-date_closed = sy-datum.
        "Save zim_in_deploy
        PERFORM save_zim_in_deploy_3300.

        w_3010_refresh = abap_true.

        MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The employee has been referred'
                          'to the FTCTL for processing'.

      ELSE.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH
          'SIMA must sign and review the IMA Report.'.
      ENDIF.


    WHEN 'NOT_SIMA'.
      "Send Reminder to SIMA that the Deployment and IMA
      "Report has been completed and Cmnd Rep has been
      "imported

      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc
         WHERE pernr        = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident     = zim_in_mast-incident
           AND doc_unique   = 'DEPREP001'.

      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc
         WHERE pernr        = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident     = zim_in_mast-incident
           AND doc_unique   = 'IMAREP001'.

        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          IF zim_in_deploy-comd_rep_recd_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
            SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_advisor
              WHERE ima = zim_in_mast-ima.
            IF sy-subrc NE 0.
              CLEAR zim_advisor.
            ENDIF.

            PERFORM create_reminder USING 'S'
                                          zim_advisor-sima
                                          '20'
                                          '11'
                                          ' '
                                          '00000000'.

            MESSAGE i999 WITH 'The SIMA has been notified'
                              'of the Report'.

          ELSE.
            MESSAGE i999 WITH
              'The Command Report needs to be imported.'.
          ENDIF.
        ELSE.
          MESSAGE i999 WITH
            'The IMA Report needs to be completed'.

        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        MESSAGE i999 WITH
          'Deployment Report needs to be completed'.

      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'LINKDOC'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_LINKDOC'
        EXPORTING
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
*         i_doclink  = 'F'.
          i_doclink  = 'A'.

    WHEN 'LINKDOC1'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'Z_OLIMS_LINKDOC'
        EXPORTING
          i_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
          i_incident = zim_in_mast-incident
          i_doclink  = 'A'.

    WHEN OTHERS.
      "Do Nothing

  ENDCASE.

ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3311  INPUT

*       Control screen I/O fields for display only
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_3415 OUTPUT.
  LOOP AT SCREEN.
*Set all fields to Display Only
    IF w_3400_action = 'DISP' AND
      screen-group4 NE '999'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

*<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03 - Primary claim maintainance only via WC screen
    IF w_epss = abap_true AND
       w_4100_from = 3010 AND
      screen-group4 NE '999'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
*>>> AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03
* Set field to invisible
    IF ( sy-tcode      = 'ZWC_GAO' OR
         w_3400_action = 'DISP'    OR
         ( w_claim_type  = 'I' OR
           w_claim_type  = 'E' OR w_claim_type  = 'F' ) ) AND "*<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03
       screen-group4 NE '999'.
      screen-invisible = '1'.
      screen-active = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_3415  OUTPUT

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  SCREEN_CONTROL_3417  OUTPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE screen_control_3417 OUTPUT.
  LOOP AT SCREEN.

**   Enable/Disable 'Create Default' button
*    IF screen-name = 'BTN_DEF'.
*      IF w_claim_type = 'W'.
*        screen-active = '0'.
*      ELSEIF w_record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_top AND
*       gr_ip_rec->get_ip_top( ) IS INITIAL.
*        screen-active = '1'.
*      ELSEIF w_record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_ip AND
*       gr_ip_rec->get_ip_ip( ) IS INITIAL.
*        screen-active = '1'.
*      ELSEIF w_record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_dis AND
*       gr_ip_rec->get_ip_dis( ) IS INITIAL.
*        screen-active = '1'.
*      ELSE.
*        screen-active = '0'.
*      ENDIF.
*      MODIFY SCREEN.
*    ENDIF.

*   End date to be disabled in case of date of disablement
    IF screen-name = 'ZIM_IN_IP-ENDDA' AND
      w_record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_dis.
      screen-input = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

*  set fields invisible based on dropdown
    IF screen-group1 IS  NOT INITIAL.
      IF w_record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_top AND       "Top-up
         screen-group1 NE 'TOP'.
        screen-active = '0'.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ELSEIF w_record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_ip AND    "IP
         screen-group1 NE 'IPP'.
        screen-active = '0'.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ELSEIF w_record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_dis AND   "Disablement
         screen-group1 NE 'DIS'.
        screen-active = '0'.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
*<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03 - Handling new record type EPSS
      ELSEIF w_record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_epss_p1 AND    "EPSS p1
         screen-group1 NE 'TOP'.
        screen-active = '0'.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ELSEIF w_record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_epss_p2 AND    "EPSS p2
         screen-group1 NE 'IPP'.
        screen-active = '0'.
        screen-invisible = '1'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
*>>> AZORBA
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

*   IP Dates record can't be more than 1, so diable new record creation when IP record exists
    IF w_record_type EQ zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_ip AND
       gr_ip_rec->get_ip_ip( ) IS NOT INITIAL AND
       screen-group4 NE '999'.
      screen-input  = '1'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

*<<< AZORBA - EPSS BRD 03
*   Disable the new record type creation as the EPSS periods are dynamically calculated in the EPSS Calculations tab
    IF w_epss EQ abap_true AND screen-group4 NE '999'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
*>>> AZORBA

    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF w_record_type IS INITIAL AND
      screen-group4 NE '999'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
    "Set all fields to Display Only
    IF w_3400_action = 'DISP' AND
      screen-group4 NE '999'.
      screen-input  = '0'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_claim_type = 'W' AND
       screen-group2 = 'WC1'.
*      sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGRCM'.
      screen-active = '0'.
      screen-invisible = '1'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

ENDMODULE.                 " SCREEN_CONTROL_3417  OUTPUT

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  CHANGE_REC_TYPE  INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE change_rec_type INPUT.
  CLEAR   zim_in_ip.
  IF zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim = abap_true.
    IF w_record_type = zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_top OR
       w_record_type = zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_dis OR
        w_record_type =  zcl_olims_ip_rec=>gc_rec_type_ip.
      MESSAGE 'Only IP Rcords allowed for Unsworn'  TYPE 'S'
      DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
* proceed with changes in ALV data

  IF gr_ip_rec IS INITIAL.
    CREATE OBJECT gr_ip_rec
      EXPORTING
        iv_pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
        iv_incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
  ENDIF.



  gr_ip_rec->set_current_record_type( iv_rec_type = w_record_type ).
  zim_in_ip-record_type = w_record_type.
  PERFORM fill_outtab_3417.
  PERFORM alv_table_refresh_3417  USING  space.
ENDMODULE.                 " CHANGE_REC_TYPE  INPUT
